<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use App\Models\PerongkosanEventLuarKota;
use App\Models\PlafonEventLuarKota;
use App\Models\EventHourGroup;
use App\Models\Wilayah;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\PlafonService;
use App\Notifications\TransaksiOverPlafon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class PerongkosanEventLuarKotaController extends Controller
{
    public function create()
    {
        $jam_kerja = EventHourGroup::all();
        return view('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.create', compact('jam_kerja'));
    }

    public function getProvinsiByTeritory(Request $request)
    {
        $teritory = $request->get('teritory');
        $provinsis = Wilayah::where('teritory', $teritory)
                       ->whereNull('parent_id')
                       ->select('id', 'nama')
                       ->orderBy('nama', 'asc')
                       ->get();
        return response()->json($provinsis);
    }

    public function getKotaByProvinsi(Request $request)
    {
        $provinsiId = $request->get('provinsi_id');
        $kotas = Wilayah::where('parent_id', $provinsiId)
                    ->select('id', 'nama')
                    ->orderBy('nama', 'asc')
                    ->get();
        return response()->json($kotas);
    }

    public function getPlafonInfo(Request $request, PlafonService $plafonService)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'tanggal'            => 'required|date',
            'is_libur'           => 'required|in:0,1',
            'jam_kerja_event_id' => 'required|exists:event_hour_group,id',
        ]);

        $user = Auth::user();

        $plafon = $plafonService->getPlafonEvent(
            PlafonEventLuarKota::class,
            $user,
            $request->tanggal,
            (bool) $request->is_libur,
            $request->jam_kerja_event_id
        );

        if ($plafon) {
            return response()->json([
                'found'        => true,
                'plafon_value' => (float) $plafon->default_value,
                'hari_event'   => $plafon->hari_event,
                'message'      => '✅ Plafon ditemukan: Rp ' . number_format($plafon->default_value, 0, ',', '.'),
            ]);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'found'        => false,
            'plafon_value' => 0,
            'message'      => '⚠️ Plafon untuk Level, Kelompok Hari Kerja, Hari Event & Jam Kerja ini belum disetting.',
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request, PlafonService $plafonService)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'tanggal'            => 'required|date',
            'lokasi_event_id'    => 'required|exists:wilayah,id',
            'teritory'           => 'required|in:Jawa,Luar Jawa',
            'is_libur'           => 'required|in:0,1',
            'jam_kerja_event_id' => 'required|exists:event_hour_group,id',
            'description'        => 'required|string|max:255',
            'nominal'            => 'required|numeric|min:1',
            'foto_event_1'       => 'required|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
            'foto_event_2'       => 'nullable|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
            'foto_event_3'       => 'nullable|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
        ]);

        $lokasi = Wilayah::find($request->lokasi_event_id);
        if ($lokasi && $lokasi->teritory !== $request->teritory) {
            return redirect()->back()
                ->withErrors(['lokasi_event_id' => '❌ Lokasi event tidak sesuai dengan Territory yang dipilih!'])
                ->withInput();
        }

        $data = $request->except(['foto_event_1', 'foto_event_2', 'foto_event_3', 'teritory']);
        $data['user_id'] = Auth::id();
        $data['jarak_km'] = 0;
        $data['durasi_jam'] = 0;

        $data['periode'] = app(\App\Services\PeriodService::class)->ofDate($request->tanggal);

        $prefix = 'EV-LKR-';
        $datePrefix = \App\Support\AppTime::now()->format('ymd');
        $todayCount = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::where('transaksi_id', 'like', $prefix . $datePrefix . '%')->count();
        $sequence = str_pad($todayCount + 1, 3, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
        $data['transaksi_id'] = $prefix . $datePrefix . '-' . $sequence;

        foreach (['foto_event_1', 'foto_event_2', 'foto_event_3'] as $fotoField) {
            if ($request->hasFile($fotoField)) {
                $data[$fotoField] = $request->file($fotoField)->store('events_luar_kota', 'public');
            }
        }

        $event = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::create($data);
        $user = Auth::user();

        $cek = $plafonService->checkEvent(
            PlafonEventLuarKota::class,
            $user, $event->tanggal, (bool) $event->is_libur,
            $event->jam_kerja_event_id, $event->nominal
        );

        $wasOver = false;

        $event->update([
            'is_over_plafon' => $cek['is_over'] ? 1 : 0,
            'plafon_value'   => $cek['plafon_value'],
            'selisih_plafon' => $cek['selisih'],
        ]);

        if ($cek['is_over'] && !$wasOver && $user->atasan_user_id) {
            $atasan = User::find($user->atasan_user_id);
            $atasan?->notify(new TransaksiOverPlafon(
                $event->transaksi_id . ' (Event Luar Kota)',
                $event->nominal, $cek['plafon_value'], $cek['selisih'],
                route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index')
            ));
        }

        // 🎯 TRIGGER CASH FLOW
        event(new \App\Events\CashFlowChanged($event->user_id, $event->periode));

        return redirect()->route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index')
            ->with('success', 'Transaksi Event Luar Kota (' . $event->transaksi_id . ') berhasil disimpan!');
    }

    public function index()
    {
        $query = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::with(['user', 'wilayah', 'jamKerja']);

        if (!auth()->user()->canSeeAllTransactions()) {
            $query->where('user_id', auth()->id());
        }

        $events = $query->orderBy('tanggal', 'desc')->paginate(10);

        return view('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index', compact('events'));
    }

    public function show($id)
    {
        $event = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::with(['user', 'wilayah', 'jamKerja'])->findOrFail($id);
        abort_unless(auth()->user()->canAccessTransaction($event), 403, 'Anda tidak memiliki akses untuk melihat transaksi ini.');

        return view('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.show', compact('event'));
    }

    public function edit($id)
    {
        $event = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::with('wilayah')->findOrFail($id);

        if (!auth()->user()->canAccessTransaction($event)) {
            abort(403, 'Anda tidak memiliki akses untuk mengedit transaksi ini.');
        }

        $jam_kerja = EventHourGroup::all();
        return view('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.edit', compact('event', 'jam_kerja'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id, PlafonService $plafonService)
    {
        $event = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::findOrFail($id);

        if (!auth()->user()->canAccessTransaction($event)) {
            abort(403, 'Anda tidak memiliki akses untuk mengubah transaksi ini.');
        }

        $request->validate([
            'tanggal'            => 'required|date',
            'lokasi_event_id'    => 'required|exists:wilayah,id',
            'teritory'           => 'required|in:Jawa,Luar Jawa',
            'is_libur'           => 'required|in:0,1',
            'jam_kerja_event_id' => 'required|exists:event_hour_group,id',
            'description'        => 'required|string|max:255',
            'nominal'            => 'required|numeric|min:1',
            'foto_event_1'       => 'nullable|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
            'foto_event_2'       => 'nullable|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
            'foto_event_3'       => 'nullable|image|mimes:jpeg,png,jpg|max:2048',
        ]);

        $lokasi = Wilayah::find($request->lokasi_event_id);
        if ($lokasi && $lokasi->teritory !== $request->teritory) {
            return redirect()->back()
                ->withErrors(['lokasi_event_id' => '❌ Lokasi event tidak sesuai dengan Territory yang dipilih!'])
                ->withInput();
        }

        $data = $request->except(['foto_event_1', 'foto_event_2', 'foto_event_3', 'teritory']);
        $data['lokasi_event_id'] = $request->lokasi_event_id;
        $data['user_id'] = $event->user_id;
        $data['jarak_km'] = $event->jarak_km;
        $data['durasi_jam'] = $event->durasi_jam;

        foreach (['foto_event_1', 'foto_event_2', 'foto_event_3'] as $field) {
            if ($request->hasFile($field)) {
                if ($event->$field && Storage::disk('public')->exists($event->$field)) {
                    $oldPhoto = $event->$field;
                    DB::afterCommit(fn () => Storage::disk('public')->delete($oldPhoto));
                }
                $data[$field] = $request->file($field)->store('events_luar_kota', 'public');
            }
        }

        // 🎯 Simpan periode LAMA sebelum update
        $oldPeriode = app(\App\Services\PeriodService::class)->ofDate($event->tanggal);

        $wasOver = (bool) $event->is_over_plafon;
        $event->update($data);
        $event->forceFill(['status' => 'DRAFT', 'validated_at' => null, 'validated_by' => null,
            'submission_version' => $event->submission_version + 1])->save();

        // Hitung ulang periode berdasarkan tanggal baru
        $newPeriode = substr($event->tanggal, 0, 7);
        if ($newPeriode !== $event->periode) {
            $event->update(['periode' => $newPeriode]);
        }

        $user = $event->user;
        $cek = $plafonService->checkEvent(
            PlafonEventLuarKota::class,
            $user, $event->tanggal, (bool) $event->is_libur,
            $event->jam_kerja_event_id, $event->nominal
        );

        $event->update([
            'is_over_plafon' => $cek['is_over'] ? 1 : 0,
            'plafon_value'   => $cek['plafon_value'],
            'selisih_plafon' => $cek['selisih'],
        ]);

        if ($cek['is_over'] && !$wasOver && $user->atasan_user_id) {
            $atasan = User::find($user->atasan_user_id);
            $atasan?->notify(new TransaksiOverPlafon(
                $event->transaksi_id . ' (Event Luar Kota)',
                $event->nominal, $cek['plafon_value'], $cek['selisih'],
                route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index')
            ));
        }

        // 🎯 TRIGGER CASH FLOW — periode baru
        event(new \App\Events\CashFlowChanged($event->user_id, $event->periode));

        // 🎯 Jika periode berubah, recalculate periode LAMA juga
        if ($oldPeriode !== $event->periode) {
            event(new \App\Events\CashFlowChanged($event->user_id, $oldPeriode));
        }

        return redirect()->route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index')->with('success', 'Data berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    public function destroy($id)
    {
        $event = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::findOrFail($id);

        if (!auth()->user()->canAccessTransaction($event)) {
            abort(403, 'Anda tidak memiliki akses untuk menghapus transaksi ini.');
        }

        // 🎯 Simpan data untuk trigger SEBELUM dihapus
        $userId = $event->user_id;
        $periode = app(\App\Services\PeriodService::class)->ofDate($event->tanggal);

        foreach (['foto_event_1', 'foto_event_2', 'foto_event_3'] as $field) {
            if ($event->$field && Storage::disk('public')->exists($event->$field)) {
                $oldPhoto = $event->$field;
                DB::afterCommit(fn () => Storage::disk('public')->delete($oldPhoto));
            }
        }

        $event->delete();

        // 🎯 TRIGGER CASH FLOW
        event(new \App\Events\CashFlowChanged($userId, $periode));

        return redirect()->route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.index')->with('success', 'Data berhasil dihapus!');
    }

    public function export(Request $request)
    {
        $periode = $request->get('periode');

        $query = PerongkosanEventLuarKota::with(['user', 'wilayah']);
        if (!auth()->user()->canSeeAllTransactions()) {
            $query->where('user_id', auth()->id());
        }
        if ($periode) $query->where('periode', $periode);

        $events = $query->orderBy('tanggal')->get();
        $jams = \App\Models\EventHourGroup::pluck('code', 'id');

        $headings = ['Transaksi ID', 'Tanggal', 'Periode', 'User', 'Lokasi (Provinsi)', 'Hari Libur', 'Jam Kerja Event', 'Deskripsi', 'Nominal', 'Plafon', 'Status Plafon'];
        $rows = [];
        $total = 0;

        foreach ($events as $e) {
            $total += (float) $e->nominal;
            $rows[] = [
                $e->transaksi_id,
                \Carbon\Carbon::parse($e->tanggal)->format('d/m/Y'),
                $e->periode ?? '',
                $e->user->nama ?? '',
                $e->wilayah->nama ?? '',
                $e->is_libur ? 'Ya' : 'Tidak',
                $jams[$e->jam_kerja_event_id] ?? '',
                $e->description,
                (float) $e->nominal,
                $e->plafon_value ? (float) $e->plafon_value : '',
                $e->is_over_plafon ? 'OVER' : ($e->plafon_value ? 'SESUAI' : '-'),
            ];
        }
        $rows[] = ['TOTAL', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', (float) $total, '', ''];

        return \Maatwebsite\Excel\Facades\Excel::download(
            new \App\Exports\LaporanTableExport($headings, $rows, ['I', 'J']),
            'laporan-event-luar-kota-' . ($periode ?: 'semua-periode') . '.xlsx'
        );
    }

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Services\BsUsageCheckService;
use App\Services\BsUsageCheckFormService;
use App\Services\BsUsageDashboardService;
use App\Services\BsValidationService;
use App\Services\PeriodService;
use App\Support\AppTime;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class TransactionRecapController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request, BsUsageDashboardService $dashboard)
    {
        $filters = $dashboard->filters($request);
        $type = $filters['pemakaian_jenis'] ?? null;
        unset($filters['pemakaian_jenis']);
        if ($type) {
            return redirect()->route('transaction-recap.module', ['jenis' => $type] + $filters);
        }
        unset($filters['pemakaian_page']);

        return redirect()->to(route('dashboard.transaksi', $filters).'#pemakaian-bs');
    }

    public function module(Request $request, string $jenis, BsUsageDashboardService $dashboard)
    {
        // The route fixes the module; query parameters cannot switch the list.
        $request->query->set('pemakaian_jenis', $jenis);

        return view('transaction-recap.index', $dashboard->data($request) + ['moduleType' => $jenis]);
    }

    public function show(Request $request, string $jenis, int $source, BsUsageCheckService $service, BsUsageDashboardService $dashboard, BsUsageCheckFormService $form)
{
    $transaction = $service->source($jenis, $source);
    abort_unless($request->user()->can('akses.approval-finance') || $request->user()->canSeeAllTransactions() || (int) $transaction->user_id === (int) $request->user()->id, 403);
    $dashboard->filters($request);

    $validated = $request->validate([
        'pemeriksaan' => 'sometimes|boolean',
        'hasil' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        'dari' => 'nullable|in:dashboard,laporan,pengecekan,periode,monitoring',
        'pemakaian_periode' => ['nullable', 'string', 'regex:/^$|^\d{4}-?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/D'],
        'company' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1', 'required_if:dari,monitoring'],
        'user' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1', 'required_if:dari,monitoring'],
        'page' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        'jenis_filter' => 'nullable|in:OPR,PD,EDK,ELK',
    ]);

    $transaction->load('user');
    $monitoringContext = null;

    if (($validated['dari'] ?? null) === 'monitoring') {
        $companyId = (int) $validated['company'];
        $userId = (int) $validated['user'];
        abort_unless((int) $transaction->user_id === $userId, 403, 'Konteks monitoring tidak sesuai dengan user transaksi.');
        abort_unless($transaction->user !== null && (int) $transaction->user->company_id === $companyId, 403, 'Konteks monitoring tidak sesuai dengan company user transaksi.');

        $periode = !empty($validated['pemakaian_periode']) ? app(PeriodService::class)->normalize($validated['pemakaian_periode']) : null;
        $monitoringContext = [
            'company' => $companyId, 'user' => $userId, 'periode' => $periode,
            'page' => (int) ($validated['page'] ?? 1), 'jenis_filter' => $validated['jenis_filter'] ?? null,
        ];
    }

    $fromMonitoring = $monitoringContext !== null;
    $reviewMode = $request->boolean('pemeriksaan');
    $isFinance = $request->user()->can('akses.approval-finance');
    abort_if($reviewMode && !$isFinance, 403);

    if (BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($transaction)) {
        $transaction->load('jamKerja');
        if ($jenis === 'ELK') $transaction->load('wilayah');
    }

    $items = $service->snapshot($transaction)['items'];
    $financeResult = $dashboard->sourceResult($request->user(), $jenis, $source);
    $financeHistory = $transaction->financeChecks()->with('details')->orderBy('id')->paginate(10, ['*'], 'riwayat_page')->withQueryString()->fragment('decision-history-title');
    $latestDecision = $transaction->financeChecks()->latest('id')->first();
    $editUrl = $this->editUrl($jenis, $transaction);
    $canCorrect = (int) $transaction->user_id === (int) $request->user()->id && $request->user()->can($this->modulePermission($jenis));
    $accountingPeriod = app(\App\Services\FinanceBsExpenseService::class)->period($transaction);
    $periodClosed = !app(PeriodService::class)->isOpen($accountingPeriod);
    $closingExclusion = $periodClosed ? \App\Models\PeriodClosingLog::activeExclusions($accountingPeriod)->first(fn ($item) => $item['type'] === $jenis && (int) $item['id'] === $source) : null;
    $canReview = $isFinance && !$periodClosed && $transaction->status === 'VALIDATED' && !BsUsageCheckService::awaitingResubmission($transaction);
    $formData = $reviewMode && $canReview ? $form->data($transaction, $jenis) : null;
    $selectedDecision = $request->filled('hasil') ? $transaction->financeChecks()->with('details')->findOrFail($request->integer('hasil')) : $latestDecision?->load('details');

    return view('transaction-recap.show', compact('transaction', 'items', 'jenis', 'financeResult', 'financeHistory', 'latestDecision', 'editUrl', 'canCorrect', 'periodClosed', 'reviewMode', 'isFinance', 'canReview', 'formData', 'selectedDecision', 'closingExclusion', 'fromMonitoring', 'monitoringContext'));
}

    public function correct(Request $request, string $jenis, int $source, BsUsageCheckService $service, BsValidationService $validation)
    {
        abort_unless(in_array($jenis, ['OPR', 'PD'], true), 404);
        abort_unless($request->user()->can($this->modulePermission($jenis)), 403);
        $data = $request->validate(['check_id' => 'required|integer|min:1']);
        $transaction = $service->source($jenis, $source);
        abort_unless((int) $transaction->user_id === (int) $request->user()->id, 403);
        $validation->reopenAfterFinanceRejection($transaction, (int) $data['check_id']);

        return redirect()->to($this->editUrl($jenis, $transaction))
            ->with('success', 'Pengajuan dibuka sebagai draft perbaikan. Simpan perubahan lalu konfirmasikan kembali. Keputusan Finance sebelumnya tetap tersimpan.');
    }

    private function modulePermission(string $type): string
    {
        return match ($type) {
            'OPR' => 'akses.transaksi-operasional', 'PD' => 'akses.transaksi-perjalanan-dinas',
            'EDK' => 'akses.event-dalam-kota', 'ELK' => 'akses.event-luar-kota',
        };
    }

    private function editUrl(string $type, $source): string
    {
        return match ($type) {
            'OPR' => route('transaksi_operasional.show', $source->id).'?edit=1',
            'PD' => route('transaksi_perjalanan_dinas.show', $source->transaksi_id).'?edit=1',
            'EDK' => route('perongkosan_event_dalam_kota.edit', $source->id),
            'ELK' => route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.edit', $source->id),
        };
    }

    public function confirmEvent(Request $request, string $jenis, int $source, BsUsageCheckService $service)
    {
        abort_unless(in_array($jenis, ['EDK', 'ELK'], true), 404);
        abort_unless($request->user()->can($jenis === 'EDK' ? 'akses.event-dalam-kota' : 'akses.event-luar-kota'), 403);
        $reference = $service->source($jenis, $source);
        $period = app(PeriodService::class)->ofDate($reference->tanggal);
        DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $jenis, $source, $service, $reference, $period) {
            app(PeriodService::class)->lockOpen($period);
            DB::table('user')->where('id', $reference->user_id)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            $transaction = $service->source($jenis, $source, true);
            if ($transaction->tanggal !== $reference->tanggal || (int) $transaction->user_id !== (int) $reference->user_id) {
                throw \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException::withMessages(['source_token' => 'Event berubah. Muat ulang sebelum konfirmasi.']);
            }
            abort_unless((int) $transaction->user_id === (int) $request->user()->id, 403);
            if ($transaction->status === 'VALIDATED') return;
            $request->validate(['source_token' => 'required|string']);
            $snapshot = $service->snapshot($transaction);
            try {
                $token = json_decode(\Illuminate\Support\Facades\Crypt::decryptString($request->source_token), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
            } catch (\Illuminate\Contracts\Encryption\DecryptException|\JsonException $exception) {
                $token = null;
            }
            if ($token !== [$jenis, $source, $snapshot['fingerprint']]) {
                throw \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException::withMessages(['source_token' => 'Event berubah. Muat ulang dan periksa rincian sebelum konfirmasi.']);
            }
            $transaction->forceFill(['status' => 'VALIDATED', 'validated_at' => AppTime::now(),
                'validated_by' => $request->user()->id, 'submission_version' => $transaction->submission_version + 1])->save();
        });

        return back()->with('success', 'Pengajuan Event dikonfirmasi dan siap diperiksa Finance.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheck;
use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDetail;
use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\BsUsageCheckService;
use App\Services\BsUsageDashboardService;
use App\Services\PeriodService;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class BsUsageCheckController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $filters = $this->filters($request);
        $data = match ($filters['tampilan']) {
            'rincian' => $this->reportData($filters),
            'draft' => $this->draftData($filters),
            default => $this->sourceData($request, $filters),
        };

        return view('bs-usage-checks.index', $data + ['filters' => $filters, 'users' => $this->users()]);
    }

    public function create(string $jenis, int $source, BsUsageCheckService $service, \App\Services\BsUsageCheckFormService $form)
    {
        return view('bs-usage-checks.create', $form->data($service->source($jenis, $source), $jenis));
    }

    public function store(Request $request, string $jenis, int $source, BsUsageCheckService $service)
{
    $navigation = $request->validate([
        '_workspace' => 'sometimes|boolean',
        'workspace_context' => 'sometimes|array',
        'workspace_context.pemakaian_periode' => ['nullable', 'string', 'regex:/^$|^\d{4}-?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/D'],
        'workspace_context.pemakaian_status' => 'nullable|in:FOLLOW_UP,PENDING,APPROVED,REJECTED,REVISION,DIFFERENCE',
        'workspace_context.pemakaian_referensi' => 'nullable|string|max:64',
        'workspace_context.pemakaian_page' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        'workspace_context.dari' => 'nullable|in:dashboard,laporan,pengecekan,periode,monitoring',
        'workspace_context.company' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1', 'required_if:workspace_context.dari,monitoring'],
        'workspace_context.user' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1', 'required_if:workspace_context.dari,monitoring'],
        'workspace_context.page' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        'workspace_context.jenis_filter' => 'nullable|in:OPR,PD,EDK,ELK',
    ]);

    // Validasi konteks monitoring SEBELUM menyimpan
    if (($navigation['workspace_context']['dari'] ?? null) === 'monitoring') {
        $transaction = $service->source($jenis, $source);
        $transaction->loadMissing('user');
        $companyId = (int) ($navigation['workspace_context']['company'] ?? 0);
        $userId = (int) ($navigation['workspace_context']['user'] ?? 0);

        abort_unless($companyId > 0 && $userId > 0, 403, 'Konteks monitoring tidak lengkap.');
        abort_unless((int) $transaction->user_id === $userId, 403, 'Konteks monitoring tidak sesuai dengan user transaksi.');
        abort_unless($transaction->user !== null && (int) $transaction->user->company_id === $companyId, 403, 'Konteks monitoring tidak sesuai dengan company user transaksi.');
    }

    $check = $service->store($request->user(), $jenis, $source, $request->all());

    if ($request->boolean('_workspace')) {
        $allowedContextKeys = ['pemakaian_periode', 'pemakaian_status', 'pemakaian_referensi', 'pemakaian_page', 'dari', 'company', 'user', 'page', 'jenis_filter'];
        $context = array_intersect_key($navigation['workspace_context'] ?? [], array_flip($allowedContextKeys));
        $params = ['jenis' => $jenis, 'source' => $source];
        foreach ($context as $key => $value) { if ($value === null || $value === '') continue; $params[$key] = $value; }
        if (array_key_exists('pemakaian_periode', $context)) $params['pemakaian_periode'] = $context['pemakaian_periode'] ?? '';

        if ($check instanceof BsUsageCheckDraft) {
            return redirect()->route('transaction-recap.show', $params + ['pemeriksaan' => 1])
                ->with('success', 'Draft pemeriksaan tersimpan. Draft belum menjadi hasil Finance pada dashboard dan laporan.');
        }
        return redirect()->to(route('transaction-recap.show', $params).'#hasil-finance')
            ->with('success', 'Pengecekan '.$check->document_no.' dikonfirmasi. Sisa Saldo BS dan Total Keluar Stok Kas mengikuti hasil Finance terakhir.');
    }

    if ($check instanceof BsUsageCheckDraft) {
        return redirect()->route('bs-usage-checks.create', [$jenis, $source])->with('success', 'Draft pemeriksaan tersimpan.');
    }
    return redirect()->route('bs-usage-checks.show', $check)->with('success', 'Pengecekan '.$check->document_no.' dikonfirmasi.');
}

    public function show(BsUsageCheck $check)
    {
        $check->load(['details', 'source', 'revisionOf']);
        $history = BsUsageCheck::where('jenis_transaksi', $check->jenis_transaksi)
            ->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $check->transaksi_pemakaian_id)->with('details')
            ->orderBy('id')->paginate(10)->withQueryString()->fragment('decision-history-title');

        return view('bs-usage-checks.show', compact('check', 'history'));
    }

    public function report(Request $request)
    {
        // Keep existing bookmarks working inside the same Finance workspace.
        $request->merge(['tampilan' => 'rincian']);

        return $this->index($request);
    }

    private function filters(Request $request): array
    {
        // Saved links to the removed revision tab now show all statuses.
        if ($request->query('status_finance') === 'REVISION') {
            $request->query->set('status_finance', '');
        }
        if ($request->query('status_finance') === 'RECHECK') {
            $request->query->set('status_finance', 'PENDING');
        }

        // Tautan filter keputusan lama diarahkan ke status pengecekan.
if (in_array(
    $request->query('status_finance'),
    ['APPROVED', 'REJECTED'],
    true
)) {
    $request->query->set('status_finance', 'REVIEWED');
}

        $filters = $request->validate([
            'tampilan' => 'nullable|in:transaksi,rincian,draft',
            'tanggal_awal' => 'nullable|date_format:Y-m-d',
            'tanggal_akhir' => ['nullable', 'date_format:Y-m-d', ...($request->filled('tanggal_awal') ? ['after_or_equal:tanggal_awal'] : [])],
            'periode' => ['nullable', 'regex:/^\\d{4}-?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/D'],
            'jenis' => 'nullable|in:OPR,PD,EDK,ELK',
            'referensi' => 'nullable|string|max:64',
            'user_id' => 'nullable|integer|exists:user,id',
            'status_selisih' => 'nullable|in:sesuai,tidak_sesuai',
            'status_finance' => 'nullable|in:PENDING,REVIEWED',
            'status_cek' => 'nullable|in:sudah,belum',
            'mode' => 'nullable|in:terakhir,semua',
            'source_id' => 'nullable|required_with:jenis_source|integer|min:1',
            'jenis_source' => 'nullable|required_with:source_id|in:OPR,PD,EDK,ELK',
            'page' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
        ]);
        if (!empty($filters['periode'])) {
            $filters['periode'] = app(PeriodService::class)->normalize($filters['periode']);
        }
        $filters['tampilan'] = $filters['tampilan'] ?? 'transaksi';
        $filters['mode'] = $filters['tampilan'] === 'rincian' ? ($filters['mode'] ?? 'terakhir') : 'terakhir';
        // Normalize old lookup links to the new status filter.
        if (!$request->has('status_finance') && !empty($filters['status_cek'])) {
            $filters['status_finance'] = $filters['status_cek'] === 'belum' ? 'PENDING' : 'REVIEWED';
        }
        unset($filters['status_cek'], $filters['page']);
        if ($filters['tampilan'] !== 'transaksi') unset($filters['status_finance']);
        if ($filters['tampilan'] === 'draft') unset($filters['status_selisih']);

        return $filters;
    }

    private function sourceData(Request $request, array $filters): array
    {
        $query = app(BsUsageDashboardService::class)->financeSources($request->user());

        // ✅ PERUBAHAN 2: Hanya ambil transaksi yang sudah dikonfirmasi (VALIDATED)
        // Menggunakan 'source_status' karena ini adalah alias yang dibuat oleh BsUsageDashboardService
        $query->where('source_status', 'VALIDATED');

        foreach (['jenis' => 'jenis', 'user_id' => 'user_id', 'periode' => 'check_period', 'source_id' => 'source_id', 'jenis_source' => 'jenis'] as $filter => $column) {
            if (!empty($filters[$filter])) $query->where($column, $filters[$filter]);
        }
        if (!empty($filters['referensi'])) $query->where('no_referensi', 'like', '%'.$filters['referensi'].'%');
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_awal'])) $query->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '>=', $filters['tanggal_awal']);
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_akhir'])) $query->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '<', \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::parse($filters['tanggal_akhir'])->addDay()->toDateString());
        if (($filters['status_finance'] ?? '') === 'REVIEWED') {
    $query->whereNotNull('check_id')
        ->where('needs_recheck', 0);
} elseif (($filters['status_finance'] ?? '') === 'PENDING') {
    $query->where('finance_status', 'PENDING');
}
        if (!empty($filters['status_selisih'])) {
            // A stale comparison cannot classify the current transaction as matching.
            $query->whereNotNull('check_id')->where('needs_recheck', 0)
                ->where('different_items', $filters['status_selisih'] === 'sesuai' ? '=' : '>', 0);
        }
        $final = "finance_status IN ('APPROVED', 'REJECTED')";
        $totals = (clone $query)->selectRaw("COUNT(*) as count,
            COALESCE(SUM(nominal_user), 0) as nominal_asal,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $final THEN nominal_finance ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_disetujui,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $final THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE 0 END), 0) as selisih,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $final THEN 0 ELSE 1 END), 0) as unresolved_count")->first();
        $sources = $query->select('usage.*')
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN $final THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE NULL END as selisih")
            ->orderByDesc('tanggal')->orderBy('jenis')->orderByDesc('source_id')
            ->paginate(20)->withPath(route('bs-usage-checks.index'))->appends($filters);
        $drafts = BsUsageCheckDraft::whereIn('jenis_transaksi', $sources->pluck('jenis'))
            ->whereIn('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $sources->pluck('source_id'))
            ->get()->keyBy(fn ($draft) => $draft->jenis_transaksi.':'.$draft->transaksi_pemakaian_id);
        foreach ($sources as $source) $source->draft = $drafts->get($source->jenis.':'.$source->source_id);

        return compact('sources', 'totals');
    }

    private function draftData(array $filters): array
    {
        $query = BsUsageCheckDraft::query();
        foreach (['jenis' => 'jenis_transaksi', 'user_id' => 'user_id', 'periode' => 'periode', 'source_id' => 'transaksi_pemakaian_id', 'jenis_source' => 'jenis_transaksi'] as $filter => $column) {
            if (!empty($filters[$filter])) $query->where($column, $filters[$filter]);
        }
        if (!empty($filters['referensi'])) $query->where('no_referensi', 'like', '%'.$filters['referensi'].'%');
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_awal'])) $query->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '>=', $filters['tanggal_awal']);
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_akhir'])) $query->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '<', \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::parse($filters['tanggal_akhir'])->addDay()->toDateString());
        $drafts = $query->with(['source', 'revisionOf'])->orderByDesc('updated_at')->orderByDesc('id')
            ->paginate(20)->withPath(route('bs-usage-checks.index'))->appends($filters);

        return compact('drafts');
    }

    private function reportData(array $filters): array
    {
        $headers = BsUsageCheck::query();
        if ($filters['mode'] === 'terakhir') $headers->latestPerSource();
        foreach (['jenis' => 'jenis_transaksi', 'user_id' => 'user_id', 'periode' => 'periode', 'source_id' => 'transaksi_pemakaian_id', 'jenis_source' => 'jenis_transaksi'] as $filter => $column) {
            if (!empty($filters[$filter])) $headers->where($column, $filters[$filter]);
        }
        if (!empty($filters['referensi'])) $headers->where('no_referensi', 'like', '%'.$filters['referensi'].'%');
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_awal'])) $headers->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '>=', $filters['tanggal_awal']);
        if (!empty($filters['tanggal_akhir'])) {
            $headers->where('tanggal_pengecekan', '<', \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::parse($filters['tanggal_akhir'])->addDay()->toDateString());
        }
        $different = fn (Builder $q) => $q->whereColumn('nominal_asal', '<>', 'nominal_disetujui');
        if (($filters['status_selisih'] ?? '') === 'tidak_sesuai') {
            $headers->whereHas('details', $different);
        } elseif (($filters['status_selisih'] ?? '') === 'sesuai') {
            $headers->whereDoesntHave('details', $different);
        }

        $query = BsUsageCheckDetail::whereIn('bs_usage_check_id', $headers->select('bs_usage_checks.id'));
        $totals = (clone $query)->selectRaw('COALESCE(SUM(nominal_asal), 0) as nominal_asal, COALESCE(SUM(nominal_disetujui), 0) as nominal_disetujui, COALESCE(SUM(nominal_asal - nominal_disetujui), 0) as selisih')->toBase()->first();
        $rows = $query->with('check')->orderByDesc('bs_usage_check_id')->orderBy('id')
            ->paginate(30)->withPath(route('bs-usage-checks.index'))->appends($filters);

        return compact('rows', 'totals');
    }

    private function users()
    {
        return User::orderBy('nama')->get(['id', 'nama']);
    }
}

    <script>
        // 🎯 Logika Toggle View/Edit Mode
        function toggleEditMode() {
            const viewMode = document.getElementById('view-mode');
            const editMode = document.getElementById('edit-mode');
            
            if (viewMode.style.display === 'none') {
                // Kembali ke mode View
                viewMode.style.display = 'block';
                editMode.style.display = 'none';
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
            } else {
                // Masuk ke mode Edit
                viewMode.style.display = 'none';
                editMode.style.display = 'block';
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
                
                // Trigger cek plafon saat masuk mode edit
                ambilInfoPlafonEvent();
            }
        }

        // 🎯 Logika Disable Tombol Submit
        document.getElementById('form-event')?.addEventListener('submit', function() {
            var btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-hourglass-split me-2"></i> Sedang Menyimpan...';
        });

        // 🎯 Logika Plafon Real-time (Sama seperti sebelumnya)
        $(document).ready(function() {
            let plafonEvent = 0;
            function formatRupiah(n) { return 'Rp ' + new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID').format(n); }

            function renderPlafonEvent() {
                if (plafonEvent <= 0) { $('#card-info-plafon-event').hide(); return; }
                const nominal = parseFloat($('#nominal').val()) || 0;
                const sisa = plafonEvent - nominal;

                $('#info-plafon-event-total').text(formatRupiah(plafonEvent));
                $('#info-nominal-event').text(formatRupiah(nominal));
                $('#info-sisa-event').text(formatRupiah(sisa));

                if (sisa < 0) {
                    $('#info-sisa-event').css('color', 'var(--danger-color)');
                    $('#plafon-event-status').html('<span class="badge-soft-danger">⚠️ OVER PLAFON - Selisih: ' + formatRupiah(Math.abs(sisa)) + '</span>');
                } else {
                    $('#info-sisa-event').css('color', 'var(--success-color)');
                    $('#plafon-event-status').html('<span class="badge-soft-success">✓ Dalam Plafon - Sisa: ' + formatRupiah(sisa) + '</span>');
                }
            }

            function ambilInfoPlafonEvent() {
                const tanggal = $('#tanggal').val();
                const isLibur = $('#is_libur').val();
                const jamId   = $('#jam_kerja_event_id').val();

                if (!tanggal || isLibur === '' || isLibur === null || !jamId) {
                    plafonEvent = 0;
                    $('#card-info-plafon-event').hide();
                    return;
                }

                $.ajax({
                    url: '{{ route("api.event_dalam_kota.plafon_info") }}',
                    type: 'POST',
                    data: {
                        _token: '{{ csrf_token() }}',
                        tanggal: tanggal,
                        is_libur: isLibur,
                        jam_kerja_event_id: jamId
                    },
                    success: function(response) {
                        if (response.found) {
                            plafonEvent = response.plafon_value;
                            $('#card-info-plafon-event').show();
                            renderPlafonEvent();
                        } else {
                            plafonEvent = 0;
                            $('#card-info-plafon-event').hide();
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

            $('#tanggal, #is_libur, #jam_kerja_event_id').on('change', ambilInfoPlafonEvent);
            $('#nominal').on('input change', renderPlafonEvent);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

END FILE 020: resources/views/perongkosan_event_dalam_kota/show.blade.php

                    <div class="recap-detail-amount">Rp {{ number_format($items->sum('nominal_asal'), 2, ',', '.') }}</div>
                    <p class="recap-meta">{{ $items->count() }} rincian biaya</p>

                    @can('akses.approval-finance')
                        @if($canReview)
                            <a class="btn btn-primary w-100 mt-2" href="{{ $reviewUrl }}">
                                {{ $latestDecision ? 'Cek Ulang' : 'Periksa Pengajuan' }}
                            </a>
                        @else
                            <p class="small text-muted mt-2 mb-0">{{ $periodClosed ? 'Periode transaksi sudah ditutup.' : 'Menunggu konfirmasi pengajuan sebelum diperiksa Finance.' }}</p>
                        @endif
                    @endcan
                </section>

                <section class="recap-card mt-3">

                    <ol class="recap-step-list">
                        <li>User melengkapi rincian dan mengonfirmasi pengajuan.</li>
                        <li>Finance menyetujui atau menolak dengan alasan.</li>
                        <li>Jika ditolak, user dapat berdiskusi dengan Finance, memperbaiki transaksi, lalu mengonfirmasi pengajuan kembali.</li>
                        <li>Finance memeriksa ulang dan menyimpan keputusan baru. Riwayat keputusan tetap tersimpan.</li>
                    </ol>
                </section>

        </div>
        @if($selectedDecision)
            <section class="recap-card" id="hasil-finance" aria-labelledby="hasil-finance-title">
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">

                        <h2 id="hasil-finance-title">Rincian hasil pemeriksaan{{ $selectedDecision->id !== $latestDecision->id ? ' sebelumnya' : '' }}</h2>
                        <p class="recap-meta mb-0">{{ $selectedDecision->document_no }} · {{ $selectedDecision->checker_name }} · {{ $selectedDecision->tanggal_pengecekan->format('d/m/Y') }}</p>
                    </div>
                    @if($selectedDecision->id !== $latestDecision->id)
                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" href="{{ $detailUrl }}#hasil-finance">Hasil terbaru</a>
                    @endif
                </div>
                @if($selectedDecision->id !== $latestDecision->id || $financeResult?->needs_recheck || $transaction->status !== 'VALIDATED')
                    <p class="alert alert-info small">Ini adalah hasil pada versi pengajuan saat diperiksa. Status pengajuan saat ini mengikuti ringkasan Hasil Finance terakhir di atas.</p>
                @endif
                <p class="small"><strong>{{ \App\Services\BsUsageCheckService::decisionLabel($selectedDecision->keputusan) }}</strong>@if($selectedDecision->catatan_keputusan) · {{ $selectedDecision->catatan_keputusan }}@endif</p>
                @include('bs-usage-checks.detail-items', ['check' => $selectedDecision, 'showInternalNotes' => $isFinance])
            </section>
        @endif
        @include('transaction-recap.decision-history')
        @endif
    </div>
@endsection

BEGIN FILE 029: tests/Concerns/FinanceMonitoringScenarios.php
SHA-256: ecec83b3884d11a5961bb31bc84e6f58601ef5313e9d09f582044f0e9bd9c5cf
Ukuran sumber: 12638 byte

<?php

namespace Tests\Concerns;

use App\Models\Company;
use App\Models\Periode;
use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalHeader;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\BsUsageCheckService;

trait FinanceMonitoringScenarios
{
    private function prepareMonitoringCompanies(): array
    {
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 9, 16)->setTime(10, 0));
        Periode::query()->updateOrCreate(['periode' => '2026-09'], ['status' => 'OPEN']);

        $alpha = Company::query()->updateOrCreate(['code' => 'MON-A'], ['nama' => 'Monitoring Alpha', 'is_active' => 1]);
        $beta = Company::query()->updateOrCreate(['code' => 'MON-B'], ['nama' => 'Monitoring Beta', 'is_active' => 1]);

        User::findOrFail(1)->update(['company_id' => $alpha->id, 'is_active' => 1]);
        User::findOrFail(2)->update(['company_id' => $alpha->id, 'is_active' => 1]);
        User::findOrFail(3)->update(['company_id' => $beta->id, 'is_active' => 1]);

        User::findOrFail(2)->givePermissionTo('akses.approval-finance');
        User::findOrFail(3)->syncPermissions([]);

        return [$alpha, $beta];
    }

    private function createApprovedOperationalExpense(int $userId, string $amount = '100000.00'): TransaksiOperasionalHeader
    {
        $bs = $this->bs(['user_id' => $userId, 'nominal_approved' => '1000000.00']);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, 'VALIDATED', $amount, false, ['user_id' => $userId]);
        $detail = $source->details()->firstOrFail();
        $this->dashboardFinanceCheck($source, 'OPR', [(int) $detail->cost_category_id => $amount]);
        return $source->fresh();
    }

    private function escaped(string $value): string { return htmlspecialchars($value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); }

    private function monitoringWorkspaceContext(Company $company, int $userId, string $periode = '2026-09', int $page = 1, ?string $jenisFilter = 'OPR'): array
    {
        $context = ['pemakaian_periode' => $periode, 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $company->id, 'user' => $userId, 'page' => $page];
        if ($jenisFilter !== null) $context['jenis_filter'] = $jenisFilter;
        return $context;
    }

    private function monitoringCheckPayload($source, string $type, array $context, bool $draft = false): array
    {
        $service = app(BsUsageCheckService::class);
        $snapshot = $service->snapshot($source);
        $detail = $source->details()->firstOrFail();

        $payload = [
            'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-09-16',
            'source_token' => $service->token($type, $source->id, $snapshot['fingerprint']),
            'details' => [['source_detail_id' => $detail->id, 'cost_category_id' => $detail->cost_category_id, 'nominal_disetujui' => $detail->nominal, 'catatan' => 'Diperiksa dari monitoring']],
            '_workspace' => true,
            'workspace_context' => $context,
        ];

        if ($draft) {
            $form = $this->get(route('bs-usage-checks.create', [$type, $source->id]))->assertOk();
            $payload['save_action'] = 'draft';
            $payload['review_token'] = $form->viewData('reviewToken');
        }
        return $payload;
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_requires_approval_finance_permission(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Monitoring Alpha');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(3));
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.index'))->assertForbidden();
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_shows_all_companies_including_inactive(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $beta->update(['is_active' => 0]);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Monitoring Alpha')->assertSee('Monitoring Beta')->assertSee('Nonaktif');
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_company_page_shows_inactive_users_and_aggregates_all_users(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        User::findOrFail(3)->update(['company_id' => $alpha->id, 'is_active' => 0]);
        $this->createApprovedOperationalExpense(1, '100000.00');
        $this->createApprovedOperationalExpense(3, '200000.00');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $response = $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.company', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'periode' => '2026-09']));
        $response->assertOk()->assertSee('Monitoring Alpha')->assertSee(User::findOrFail(1)->nama)->assertSee(User::findOrFail(3)->nama)->assertSee('Nonaktif');
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_rejects_cross_company_user_url(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 3, 'periode' => '2026-09']))->assertForbidden();
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $beta->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09']))->assertForbidden();
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_user_page_paginates_transactions_from_query(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $bs = $this->bs(['user_id' => 1, 'nominal_approved' => '1000000.00']);
        for ($i = 0; $i < 25; $i++) $this->expense($bs, 'VALIDATED', '1000.00', false, ['user_id' => 1]);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $responsePage1 = $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'page' => 1]));
        $paginatorPage1 = $responsePage1->viewData('transactions');
        $this->assertSame(25, $paginatorPage1->total());
        $this->assertSame(20, $paginatorPage1->count());
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_validates_page_and_period_input(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->getJson(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'page' => 'abc']))->assertStatus(422)->assertJsonValidationErrors('page');
        $this->getJson(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => ['invalid']]))->assertStatus(422)->assertJsonValidationErrors('periode');
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_shows_current_finance_decision_not_old_decision_after_revision(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $source = $this->createApprovedOperationalExpense(1, '100000.00');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09']))->assertOk()->assertSee('Disetujui');
        $source->increment('submission_version');
        $this->get(route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09']))->assertOk()->assertSee('Perlu Dicek Ulang')->assertDontSee('Disetujui');
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_detail_transaction_preserves_monitoring_context(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $source = $this->createApprovedOperationalExpense(1, '100000.00');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $detailUrl = route('transaction-recap.show', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id, 'pemakaian_periode' => '2026-09', 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'page' => 1]);
        $response = $this->get($detailUrl);
        $expectedBackUrl = route('monitoring-finance.user', ['company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'page' => 1]);
        $response->assertOk()->assertSee('href="' . $this->escaped($expectedBackUrl) . '#monitoring-transactions"', false);
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_detail_transaction_rejects_invalid_context(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $source = $this->createApprovedOperationalExpense(1, '100000.00');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->get(route('transaction-recap.show', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id, 'pemakaian_periode' => '2026-09', 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $beta->id, 'user' => 1, 'page' => 1]))->assertForbidden();
        $this->get(route('transaction-recap.show', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id, 'pemakaian_periode' => '2026-09', 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 3, 'page' => 1]))->assertForbidden();
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_can_store_check_from_monitoring_workspace(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $bs = $this->bs(['user_id' => 1, 'nominal_approved' => '1000000.00']);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, 'VALIDATED', '100000.00', false, ['user_id' => 1]);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $response = $this->post(route('bs-usage-checks.store', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id]), $this->monitoringCheckPayload($source, 'OPR', $this->monitoringWorkspaceContext($alpha, 1), false));
        $expected = route('transaction-recap.show', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id, 'pemakaian_periode' => '2026-09', 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'page' => 1, 'jenis_filter' => 'OPR']);
        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors()->assertRedirect($expected . '#hasil-finance');
        $this->assertSame(1, \App\Models\BsUsageCheck::query()->where('jenis_transaksi', 'OPR')->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $source->id)->count());
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_store_rejects_mismatched_context_before_persisting(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $bs = $this->bs(['user_id' => 1, 'nominal_approved' => '1000000.00']);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, 'VALIDATED', '100000.00', false, ['user_id' => 1]);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $validContext = $this->monitoringWorkspaceContext($alpha, 1);
        $wrongCompany = $validContext; $wrongCompany['company'] = $beta->id;
        $wrongUser = $validContext; $wrongUser['user'] = 3;

        foreach ([false, true] as $draft) {
            foreach ([$wrongCompany, $wrongUser] as $context) {
                $this->post(route('bs-usage-checks.store', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id]), $this->monitoringCheckPayload($source, 'OPR', $context, $draft))->assertForbidden();
                $this->assertSame(0, \App\Models\BsUsageCheck::query()->count(), 'Keputusan Finance tidak boleh tersimpan saat konteks salah.');
                $this->assertSame(0, \App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft::query()->count(), 'Draft tidak boleh tersimpan saat konteks salah.');
                $this->assertSame('1000000.00', app(\App\Services\BonSementaraService::class)->getRealtimeSisaSaldo(1, '2026-09'), 'Saldo BS tidak boleh berubah saat konteks salah.');
            }
        }
    }

    public function test_finance_monitoring_can_save_draft_from_monitoring_workspace(): void
    {
        [$alpha] = $this->prepareMonitoringCompanies();
        $bs = $this->bs(['user_id' => 1, 'nominal_approved' => '1000000.00']);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, 'VALIDATED', '100000.00', false, ['user_id' => 1]);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $response = $this->post(route('bs-usage-checks.store', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id]), $this->monitoringCheckPayload($source, 'OPR', $this->monitoringWorkspaceContext($alpha, 1), true));
        $expected = route('transaction-recap.show', ['jenis' => 'OPR', 'source' => $source->id, 'pemakaian_periode' => '2026-09', 'dari' => 'monitoring', 'company' => $alpha->id, 'user' => 1, 'page' => 1, 'jenis_filter' => 'OPR', 'pemeriksaan' => 1]);
        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors()->assertRedirect($expected);
        $this->assertSame(1, \App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft::query()->where('jenis_transaksi', 'OPR')->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $source->id)->count());
        $this->assertSame(0, \App\Models\BsUsageCheck::query()->count());
        $this->assertSame('1000000.00', app(\App\Services\BonSementaraService::class)->getRealtimeSisaSaldo(1, '2026-09'));
    }
}

<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Http\Middleware\CheckActiveAtasan;
use App\Http\Middleware\CheckActiveCompany;
use App\Http\Middleware\InjectNotificationBell;
use App\Models\BsCashFlowSnapshot;
use App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot;
