<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

class PengajuanBs extends Model
{
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'pengajuan_bs';

    protected $guarded = ['id'];

    protected $casts = [
        'tanggal'    => 'datetime',
        'settled_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /* ================= RELATIONS ================= */

    public function user()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'user_id');
    }

    public function atasan()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'atasan_user_id');
    }

    public function members()
    {
        return $this->belongsToMany(User::class, 'pengajuan_bs_members', 'pengajuan_bs_id', 'user_id');
    }

    public function financeApprovals()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ApprovalBsFinance::class, 'pengajuan_bs_id');
    }

    public function operasionalSales()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class, 'bs_id');
    }

    public function perjalananDinas()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class, 'bs_id');
    }

    public function logs()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(BsActivityLog::class, 'pengajuan_bs_id');
    }

    /* ================= ACCESSORS ================= */

    public function isRejectedBySystem(): bool
    {
        if ($this->status !== 'REJECTED_ATASAN') return false;

        return $this->relationLoaded('logs')
            ? $this->logs->contains('activity_code', 'REJECTED_SYSTEM')
            : $this->logs()->where('activity_code', 'REJECTED_SYSTEM')->exists();
    }

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return $this->isRejectedBySystem() ? 'Ditolak Sistem' : str_replace('_', ' ', $this->status);
    }

    /** Label tampilan tipe pengajuan */
    public function getTipeLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return $this->tipe_pengajuan === 'perjalanan_dinas'
            ? 'Perjalanan Dinas'
            : 'Operasional Sales';
    }

    /** Apakah BS ini tipe perjalanan dinas? */
    public function isPerjalananDinas(): bool
    {
        return $this->tipe_pengajuan === 'perjalanan_dinas';
    }
}

BEGIN FILE 040: app/Models/Concerns/UsesBsBalance.php
SHA-256: 34cc29f7a4fd975649c3215ec258eaa6b55bc788ac10c354da99216596538694
Ukuran sumber: 1051 byte
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<?php

namespace App\Models\Concerns;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;

trait UsesBsBalance
{
    public function bsOwner()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'bs_owner_id');
    }

    public function bsCashOwnerId(): ?int
    {
        $owner = $this->bs_owner_id ?? $this->bonSementara?->user_id;
        return $owner === null ? null : (int) $owner;
    }

    public function scopeForBsOwner(Builder $query, int $ownerId): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(fn ($q) => $q->where('bs_owner_id', $ownerId)
            ->orWhere(fn ($legacy) => $legacy->whereNull('bs_owner_id')
                ->whereHas('bonSementara', fn ($bs) => $bs->where('user_id', $ownerId))));
    }

    public function scopeVisibleTo(Builder $query, User $user): Builder
    {
        return $query->when(!$user->canSeeAllTransactions(), fn ($q) => $q
            ->where(fn ($visible) => $visible->where('user_id', $user->id)
                ->orWhere(fn ($owned) => $owned->forBsOwner((int) $user->id))));
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BsUsageCheck extends Model
{
    protected $guarded = ['id'];

    protected $casts = ['tanggal_pengecekan' => 'date'];

    public function user()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class);
    }

    public function checker()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'user_checker_id');
    }

    public function source()
    {
        return $this->morphTo(__FUNCTION__, 'jenis_transaksi', 'transaksi_pemakaian_id');
    }

    public function details()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(BsUsageCheckDetail::class);
    }

    public function revisionOf()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(self::class, 'revision_of_id');
    }

    public function scopeLatestPerSource(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        // Choose the globally latest saved check BEFORE date/status filters.
        return $query->whereIn('bs_usage_checks.id', DB::table('bs_usage_checks')
            ->selectRaw('MAX(id)')->groupBy('jenis_transaksi', 'transaksi_pemakaian_id'));
    }

    public function scopeDifferent(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->whereHas('details', fn ($detail) => $detail->whereColumn('nominal_asal', '<>', 'nominal_disetujui'));
    }
}

BEGIN FILE 046: app/Services/StockCashFlowService.php
SHA-256: 66da850f68a7dbd722ea0e682febf160230177ce4171be976e14f937ac52bd69
Ukuran sumber: 11251 byte
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\ApprovalBsFinance;
use App\Models\BsCashFlowSnapshot;
use App\Models\PengajuanBs;
use App\Models\Periode;
use App\Models\PerongkosanEventDalamKota;
use App\Models\PerongkosanEventLuarKota;
use App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot;
use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalHeader;
use App\Models\TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader;
use App\Support\Money;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class StockCashFlowService
{
    private const EVENTS = [
        PerongkosanEventDalamKota::class => 'Event Dalam Kota',
        PerongkosanEventLuarKota::class => 'Event Luar Kota',
    ];

    public function __construct(private PeriodService $periods, private BsCashFlowService $bsCash) {}

    /** Include actual posting periods even when a period or snapshot has not been created yet. */
    public function dashboardPeriods(int $userId): Collection
    {
        $periods = Periode::pluck('periode')
            ->merge(StockCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->pluck('periode'))
            ->merge(BsCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->pluck('periode'))
            ->merge(PengajuanBs::where('user_id', $userId)->pluck('settled_periode'));

        $receiptDates = ApprovalBsFinance::whereHas('pengajuanBs', fn ($query) => $query->where('user_id', $userId))
            ->distinct()->pluck('tanggal_cair');
        $periods = $periods->merge($receiptDates->map(fn ($date) => substr((string) $date, 0, 7)));

        foreach ([TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class, TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class] as $model) {
            $periods = $periods->merge($model::forBsOwner($userId)->distinct()->pluck('periode'));
        }
        foreach (array_keys(self::EVENTS) as $model) {
            $dates = $model::where('user_id', $userId)->distinct()->pluck('tanggal');
            $periods = $periods->merge($dates->map(fn ($date) => substr((string) $date, 0, 7)));
        }

        return $periods->push($this->periods->current())->filter()
            ->map(fn ($period) => $this->periods->normalize($period))
            ->unique()->sort()->values();
    }

    /** Empty period means all history: count the opening once, never sum carried balances. */
    public function dashboardSummary(int $userId, string $period, bool $refreshSnapshots = true): object
    {
        $read = function (string $month) use ($userId, $refreshSnapshots): object {
            if ($refreshSnapshots) return $this->recalculateSnapshot($userId, $month);
            // Users without Stok Kas access can read their balance without creating cash reports.
            $balance = $this->bsCash->calculate($userId, $month);
            return (object) ($balance + ['saldo_berjalan' => $this->bsCash->availableBalance($balance)]);
        };
        if ($period !== '') return $read($period);

        $snapshots = $this->dashboardPeriods($userId)->map($read);
        // Empty periods before this user's ledger starts must not hide its initial balance.
        $first = $snapshots->first(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row->saldo_awal) !== 0
            || Money::minor($row->total_masuk) !== 0 || Money::minor($row->total_keluar) !== 0
            || Money::minor($row->penyesuaian) !== 0) ?? $snapshots->first();
        $opening = Money::minor($first->saldo_awal);
        $incoming = $snapshots->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row->total_masuk));
        $outgoing = $snapshots->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row->total_keluar));
        $adjustment = $snapshots->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row->penyesuaian));

        return (object) [
            'saldo_awal' => Money::decimal($opening),
            'total_masuk' => Money::decimal($incoming),
            'total_keluar' => Money::decimal($outgoing),
            'penyesuaian' => Money::decimal($adjustment),
            'saldo_berjalan' => Money::decimal($opening + $incoming - $outgoing + $adjustment),
            'opening_final' => (bool) $first->opening_final,
        ];
    }

    public function events(string $model, int $userId, string $periode)
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        return $model::where('user_id', $userId)
            ->where('tanggal', '>=', $periode.'-01')
            ->where('tanggal', '<', $this->periods->next($periode).'-01');
    }

    public function recalculateSnapshot(int $userId, string $periode): StockCashFlowSnapshot
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($userId, $periode) {
            $period = Periode::where('periode', $periode)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            DB::table('user')->where('id', $userId)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            $existing = StockCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->where('periode', $periode)->first();
            if ($period?->status === 'CLOSED' && $existing?->saldo_akhir !== null) {
                return $this->withArchivedBreakdown($existing);
            }

            $bs = $this->bsCash->recalculateSnapshot($userId, $periode);
            $opening = Money::minor($bs->saldo_awal);
            $ready = $bs->opening_final;
            $totals = $this->expenseTotals($userId, $periode, (bool) $bs->finance_confirmed);
            $local = $totals['EDK'];
            $outOfTown = $totals['ELK'];

            if (!$bs->finance_confirmed) {
                $previous = $this->periods->previous($periode);
                if (Periode::where('periode', $previous)->where('status', 'CLOSED')->exists()) {
                    $prior = $this->recalculateSnapshot($userId, $previous);
                    $opening = Money::minor($prior->saldo_akhir);
                    $ready = $ready && $prior->opening_final;
                }
            }

            return StockCashFlowSnapshot::updateOrCreate(
                ['user_id' => $userId, 'periode' => $periode],
                [
                    'saldo_awal' => Money::decimal($opening),
                    'total_masuk' => $bs->total_masuk,
                    'total_masuk_reimbursement' => '0.00',
                    'total_keluar_bs' => Money::decimal(Money::minor($bs->total_keluar) - ($bs->finance_confirmed ? $local + $outOfTown : 0)),
                    'total_keluar_operasional' => Money::decimal($totals['OPR']),
                    'total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas' => Money::decimal($totals['PD']),
                    'total_event_dalam' => Money::decimal($local),
                    'total_event_luar' => Money::decimal($outOfTown),
                    'total_keluar_pribadi' => '0.00',
                    'total_keluar' => Money::decimal(Money::minor($bs->total_keluar) + ($bs->finance_confirmed ? 0 : $local + $outOfTown)),
                    'includes_personal_funds' => false,
                    'penyesuaian' => $bs->penyesuaian,
                    'saldo_akhir' => $period?->status === 'CLOSED'
                        ? ($bs->finance_confirmed ? $bs->saldo_akhir : Money::decimal(Money::minor($bs->saldo_akhir) + $opening - Money::minor($bs->saldo_awal) - $local - $outOfTown))
                        : null,
                    'finance_confirmed' => (bool) $bs->finance_confirmed,
                    'opening_final' => $ready,
                    'last_updated' => now(),
                ]
            );
        }, 3);
    }

    /** Amounts in minor units, using the same Finance decisions as Total Keluar. */
    private function expenseTotals(int $userId, string $periode, bool $financeConfirmed): array
    {
        if ($financeConfirmed) {
            $expenses = app(FinanceBsExpenseService::class)->expenses($userId, $periode);
            return collect(['OPR', 'PD', 'EDK', 'ELK'])->mapWithKeys(fn ($module) => [
                $module => $expenses->where('module', $module)->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row['nominal'])),
            ])->all();
        }

        $totals = [];
        foreach (['OPR' => TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class, 'PD' => TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class] as $module => $model) {
            $totals[$module] = $this->bsCash->expenses($model, $userId, $periode)->withSum('details', 'nominal')->get()
                ->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row->details_sum_nominal));
        }
        $totals['EDK'] = Money::minor($this->events(PerongkosanEventDalamKota::class, $userId, $periode)->sum('nominal'));
        $totals['ELK'] = Money::minor($this->events(PerongkosanEventLuarKota::class, $userId, $periode)->sum('nominal'));
        return $totals;
    }

    /** Derive missing labels for display only; never rewrite published closing balances. */
    private function withArchivedBreakdown(StockCashFlowSnapshot $snapshot): StockCashFlowSnapshot
    {
        if ($snapshot->total_keluar_operasional !== null && $snapshot->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas !== null) return $snapshot;
        $totals = $this->expenseTotals((int) $snapshot->user_id, $snapshot->periode, (bool) $snapshot->finance_confirmed);
        if ($totals['OPR'] + $totals['PD'] !== Money::minor($snapshot->total_keluar_bs)) return $snapshot;
        foreach (['OPR' => 'total_keluar_operasional', 'PD' => 'total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas'] as $module => $column) {
            if ($snapshot->$column !== null && Money::minor($snapshot->$column) !== $totals[$module]) return $snapshot;
        }
        $snapshot->total_keluar_operasional = Money::decimal($totals['OPR']);
        $snapshot->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas = Money::decimal($totals['PD']);
        return $snapshot;
    }

    public function owners(): array
    {
        $owners = collect($this->bsCash->owners())->merge(StockCashFlowSnapshot::pluck('user_id'));
        foreach (array_keys(self::EVENTS) as $model) $owners = $owners->merge($model::distinct()->pluck('user_id'));
        return $owners->unique()->sort()->values()->all();
    }

    public function ensureSnapshotForPeriod(string $periode): void
    {
        foreach ($this->owners() as $owner) $this->recalculateSnapshot((int) $owner, $periode);
    }

    public function details(int $userId, string $periode): array
    {
        $snapshot = $this->recalculateSnapshot($userId, $periode);
        $result = $this->bsCash->details($userId, $periode);
        if ($snapshot->finance_confirmed) return $result;
        foreach (self::EVENTS as $model => $label) {
            foreach ($this->events($model, $userId, $periode)->orderBy('tanggal')->orderBy('id')->get() as $event) {
                $result['keluar'][] = [
                    'type' => $label,
                    'date' => $event->tanggal,
                    'description' => ($event->transaksi_id ?? 'Event #'.$event->id).($event->description ? ' / '.$event->description : ''),
                    'nominal' => $event->nominal,
                    'url' => $event instanceof PerongkosanEventDalamKota
                        ? route('perongkosan_event_dalam_kota.show', $event->id)
                        : route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.show', $event->id),
                ];
            }
        }
        $result['masuk'] = collect($result['masuk'])->sortBy('date')->values()->all();
        $result['keluar'] = collect($result['keluar'])->sortBy('date')->values()->all();
        return $result;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\ApprovalBsFinance;
use App\Models\BsCashFlowSnapshot;
use App\Models\PengajuanBs;
use App\Models\Periode;
use App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot;
use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalHeader;
use App\Models\TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader;
use App\Support\Money;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BsCashFlowService
{
    public function __construct(private PeriodService $periods) {}

    public function receipts(int $userId, string $periode)
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        return ApprovalBsFinance::whereHas('pengajuanBs', fn ($q) => $q->where('user_id', $userId))
            ->where('tanggal_cair', '>=', $periode.'-01')
            ->where('tanggal_cair', '<', $this->periods->next($periode).'-01');
    }

    public function expenses(string $model, int $userId, string $periode)
    {
        return $model::forBsOwner($userId)
            ->where('periode', $this->periods->normalize($periode))
            ->where('status', 'VALIDATED');
    }

    /** Separate existing settlement adjustments; this service does not perform settlement. */
    public function adjustment(int $userId, string $periode): int
    {
        $query = PengajuanBs::where('user_id', $userId)->where('status', 'SETTLED')->where('settled_periode', $periode);
        return Money::minor((clone $query)->sum('nominal_reimburse')) - Money::minor($query->sum('nominal_pengembalian'));
    }

    public function calculate(int $userId, string $periode): array
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        if (Periode::where('periode', $periode)->where('status', 'CLOSED')->exists()) {
            $frozen = BsCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->where('periode', $periode)->first();
            if ($frozen?->saldo_akhir !== null) {
                return $frozen->only(['saldo_awal', 'total_masuk', 'total_keluar', 'penyesuaian', 'saldo_akhir', 'opening_final', 'finance_confirmed']);
            }
        }
        $previous = $this->periods->previous($periode);
        $previousRecord = Periode::where('periode', $previous)->first();
        $opening = 0;
        $ready = !Periode::where('periode', '<', $periode)->exists();

        if ($previousRecord?->status === 'CLOSED') {
            // Continue the published cash closing balance, including historical event costs.
            // Both current ledgers must inherit it once, even if the old BS-only balance differs.
            $prior = StockCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->where('periode', $previous)
                ->whereNotNull('saldo_akhir')->first()
                ?? BsCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->where('periode', $previous)->first();
            if ($prior && $prior->saldo_akhir !== null) {
                $opening = Money::minor($prior->saldo_akhir);
                $ready = $prior->opening_final;
            } else {
                // Bootstrap a missing prior summary without rewriting existing closed snapshots.
                $prior = $this->calculate($userId, $previous);
                $opening = Money::minor($prior['saldo_akhir']);
                $ready = $prior['opening_final'];
            }
        }

        $incoming = Money::minor($this->receipts($userId, $periode)->sum('nominal_approved'));
        $outgoing = app(FinanceBsExpenseService::class)->expenses($userId, $periode)
            ->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row['nominal']));
        $adjustment = $this->adjustment($userId, $periode);

        return [
            'saldo_awal' => Money::decimal($opening),
            'total_masuk' => Money::decimal($incoming),
            'total_keluar' => Money::decimal($outgoing),
            'penyesuaian' => Money::decimal($adjustment),
            'saldo_akhir' => Periode::where('periode', $periode)->where('status', 'CLOSED')->exists()
                ? Money::decimal($opening + $incoming - $outgoing + $adjustment)
                : null,
            'opening_final' => (bool) $ready,
            'finance_confirmed' => true,
        ];
    }

    /** Funds available for transaction validation, separate from the closing balance. */
    public function availableBalance(array $balance): string
    {
        return Money::decimal(
            Money::minor($balance['saldo_awal']) + Money::minor($balance['total_masuk'])
            - Money::minor($balance['total_keluar']) + Money::minor($balance['penyesuaian'])
        );
    }

    public function recalculateSnapshot(int $userId, string $periode): BsCashFlowSnapshot
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($userId, $periode) {
            $period = Periode::where('periode', $periode)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            DB::table('user')->where('id', $userId)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            $existing = BsCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', $userId)->where('periode', $periode)->first();
            if ($period?->status === 'CLOSED' && $existing?->saldo_akhir !== null) return $existing;

            return BsCashFlowSnapshot::updateOrCreate(
                ['user_id' => $userId, 'periode' => $periode],
                $this->calculate($userId, $periode) + ['last_updated' => now()]
            );
        }, 3);
    }

    public function owners(): array
    {
        return PengajuanBs::pluck('user_id')->merge(BsCashFlowSnapshot::pluck('user_id'))
            ->merge(TransaksiOperasionalHeader::whereNotNull('bs_owner_id')->pluck('bs_owner_id'))
            ->merge(TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::whereNotNull('bs_owner_id')->pluck('bs_owner_id'))
            ->merge(\App\Models\PerongkosanEventDalamKota::pluck('user_id'))
            ->merge(\App\Models\PerongkosanEventLuarKota::pluck('user_id'))
            ->unique()->sort()->values()->all();
    }

    public function ensureSnapshotForPeriod(string $periode): void
    {
        foreach ($this->owners() as $owner) $this->recalculateSnapshot((int) $owner, $periode);
    }

    public function details(int $userId, string $periode): array
    {
        $periode = $this->periods->normalize($periode);
        $result = ['masuk' => [], 'keluar' => [], 'penyesuaian' => []];
        foreach ($this->receipts($userId, $periode)->with('pengajuanBs')->orderBy('tanggal_cair')->get() as $receipt) {
            $result['masuk'][] = ['type' => 'BS Dicairkan', 'date' => $receipt->tanggal_cair,
                'description' => $receipt->pengajuanBs?->document_no ?? '-', 'nominal' => $receipt->nominal_approved,
                'url' => route('pengajuan-bs.show', $receipt->pengajuan_bs_id)];
        }
        $snapshot = $this->recalculateSnapshot($userId, $periode);
        if ($snapshot->finance_confirmed) {
            $result['keluar'] = app(FinanceBsExpenseService::class)->expenses($userId, $periode)->all();
        } else foreach ([TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class => 'Operasional Area Sales', TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class => 'Perjalanan Dinas'] as $model => $label) {
            foreach ($this->expenses($model, $userId, $periode)->with(['user', 'bonSementara'])->withSum('details', 'nominal')->orderBy('tanggal')->get() as $header) {
                $result['keluar'][] = ['type' => $label, 'date' => $header->tanggal,
                    'description' => $header->transaksi_id.' (diinput: '.($header->user?->nama ?? '-').')',
                    'nominal' => $header->details_sum_nominal,
                    'url' => $header instanceof TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader
                        ? route('transaksi_perjalanan_dinas.show', $header->transaksi_id)
                        : route('transaksi_operasional.show', $header->id)];
            }
        }
        foreach (PengajuanBs::where('user_id', $userId)->where('status', 'SETTLED')->where('settled_periode', $periode)->get() as $bs) {
            $amount = Money::minor($bs->nominal_reimburse) - Money::minor($bs->nominal_pengembalian);
            if ($amount !== 0) $result['penyesuaian'][] = ['description' => $bs->document_no, 'nominal' => Money::decimal($amount), 'date' => $bs->settled_at?->toDateString(),
                'url' => route('pengajuan-bs.show', $bs->id)];
        }
        return $result;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheck;
use App\Support\Money;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;

/** The latest confirmed Finance decision is the single source of posted BS usage. */
class FinanceBsExpenseService
{
    public function owner(Model $source): ?int
    {
        return BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source) ? (int) $source->user_id : $source->bsCashOwnerId();
    }

    public function period(Model $source): string
    {
        $periods = app(PeriodService::class);

        return BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source)
            ? $periods->ofDate($source->tanggal) : $periods->normalize($source->periode);
    }

    public function sources(string $type, string $period, ?int $owner = null)
    {
        $model = BsUsageCheckService::TYPES[$type];
        $event = BsUsageCheckService::isEvent(new $model);
        $query = $model::query();
        if ($event) {
            $query->where('tanggal', '>=', $period.'-01')
                ->where('tanggal', '<', app(PeriodService::class)->next($period).'-01');
            if ($owner !== null) $query->where('user_id', $owner);
        } else {
            $query->where('periode', $period);
            if ($owner !== null) $query->forBsOwner($owner);
        }

        return $query->with([
            'financeChecks' => fn ($checks) => $checks->latestPerSource()->with('details'),
            ...($event ? ['user'] : ['user', 'bonSementara', 'details']),
        ]);
    }

    public function currentCheck(Model $source): ?BsUsageCheck
    {
        if ($source->status !== 'VALIDATED') return null;
        $check = $source->relationLoaded('financeChecks') ? $source->financeChecks->first()
            : $source->financeChecks()->latest('id')->with('details')->first();
        if (!$check || (int) $check->source_revision !== (int) $source->submission_version) return null;
        if (BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source) && $check->source_revision === null) return null;

        if (!BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source) && $check->details->contains(fn ($item) => $item->source_detail_id !== null)) {
            $original = $source->details->mapWithKeys(fn ($item) => [(int) $item->id => [
                (int) $item->cost_category_id, Money::minor($item->nominal), $item->remark ?? '',
            ]])->all();
            $checked = $check->details->mapWithKeys(fn ($item) => [(int) $item->source_detail_id => [
                (int) $item->cost_category_id, Money::minor($item->nominal_asal), $item->source_remark ?? '',
            ]])->all();
            ksort($original);
            ksort($checked);

            return $original === $checked ? $check : null;
        }

        // Historical category-level approvals retain their original amounts and matching rules.
        $original = BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source)
            ? [0 => Money::minor($source->nominal)]
            : $source->details->groupBy('cost_category_id')->map(fn ($items) =>
                $items->sum(fn ($item) => Money::minor($item->nominal)))->all();
        $checked = $check->details->mapWithKeys(fn ($item) =>
            [(int) $item->cost_category_id => Money::minor($item->nominal_asal)])->all();
        ksort($original);
        ksort($checked);

        return $original === $checked ? $check : null;
    }

    public function approvedAmount(Model $source): int
    {
        $check = $this->currentCheck($source);

        return $check?->keputusan === 'APPROVED'
            ? $check->details->sum(fn ($item) => Money::minor($item->nominal_disetujui)) : 0;
    }

    public function expenses(int $owner, string $period): Collection
    {
        $period = app(PeriodService::class)->normalize($period);
        $rows = collect();
        foreach (BsUsageCheckService::TYPES as $type => $model) {
            foreach ($this->sources($type, $period, $owner)->where('status', 'VALIDATED')->get() as $source) {
                $check = $this->currentCheck($source);
                if ($check?->keputusan !== 'APPROVED') continue;
                $rows->push([
                    'type' => BsUsageCheckService::LABELS[$type], 'module' => $type,
                    'date' => $source->tanggal,
                    'description' => ($source->transaksi_id ?: $type.' #'.$source->id)
                        .(BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source) && $source->description ? ' / '.$source->description : '')
                        .' (diinput: '.($source->user?->nama ?? '-').'; Finance: '.$check->document_no.')',
                    'nominal' => Money::decimal($this->approvedAmount($source)),
                    'url' => match ($type) {
                        'OPR' => route('transaksi_operasional.show', $source->id),
                        'PD' => route('transaksi_perjalanan_dinas.show', $source->transaksi_id),
                        'EDK' => route('perongkosan_event_dalam_kota.show', $source->id),
                        'ELK' => route('perongkosan_event_luar_kota.show', $source->id),
                    },
                ]);
            }
        }

        return $rows->sortBy('date')->values();
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheck;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Query\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Query\JoinClause;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BsUsageDashboardService
{
    public function filters(Request $request): array
    {
        // Keep saved links to the former status usable with the Pending filter.
        if ($request->query('pemakaian_status') === 'RECHECK') {
            $request->query->set('pemakaian_status', 'PENDING');
        }

        $filters = $request->validate([
            'pemakaian_periode' => ['nullable', 'regex:/^\d{4}-?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/D'],
            'pemakaian_jenis' => 'nullable|in:OPR,PD,EDK,ELK',
            'pemakaian_page' => 'nullable|integer|min:1',
            'pemakaian_status' => 'nullable|in:FOLLOW_UP,PENDING,APPROVED,REJECTED,REVISION,DIFFERENCE',
            'pemakaian_referensi' => 'nullable|string|max:64',
            'tampilan' => 'nullable|in:ringkasan,daftar,pengajuan,finance',
        ]);
        unset($filters['tampilan']);
        $periods = app(PeriodService::class);
        $filters['pemakaian_periode'] = $request->query->has('pemakaian_periode')
            ? (!empty($filters['pemakaian_periode']) ? $periods->normalize($filters['pemakaian_periode']) : '')
            : $periods->current();

        return $filters;
    }

    public function data(Request $request): array
    {
        $filters = $this->filters($request);
        $union = null;
        foreach (BsUsageCheckService::TYPES as $type => $model) {
            $part = $this->transactions($type, $model, $request->user());
            if (!empty($filters['pemakaian_periode'])) {
                $part->where((new $model)->getTable().'.periode', $filters['pemakaian_periode']);
            }
            if (!empty($filters['pemakaian_jenis']) && $filters['pemakaian_jenis'] !== $type) {
                $part->whereRaw('1 = 0');
            }
            $union = $union ? $union->unionAll($part) : $part;
        }
        $query = $this->classified($union);
        if (!empty($filters['pemakaian_referensi'])) $query->where('no_referensi', 'like', '%'.$filters['pemakaian_referensi'].'%');
        if (($filters['pemakaian_status'] ?? '') === 'FOLLOW_UP') {
            $query->where('in_pending_group', 1);
        } elseif (($filters['pemakaian_status'] ?? '') === 'DIFFERENCE') {
            $query->where('finance_status', 'APPROVED')->where('different_items', '>', 0);
        } elseif (!empty($filters['pemakaian_status'])) {
            $query->where('finance_status', $filters['pemakaian_status']);
        }
        $summaryQuery = $this->summary(clone $query);
        $summary = (clone $summaryQuery)->first();
        $byModule = (clone $summaryQuery)->addSelect('jenis')->groupBy('jenis')->get()->keyBy('jenis');
        $empty = (object) array_fill_keys(array_keys((array) $summary), 0);
        $modules = [];
        foreach (BsUsageCheckService::LABELS as $type => $label) $modules[$type] = $byModule->get($type, clone $empty);
        $rows = $query->select('usage.*')
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN check_id IS NOT NULL AND needs_recheck = 0 AND keputusan <> 'REVISION' THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE NULL END as selisih")
            ->orderByRaw('COALESCE(validated_at, tanggal) DESC')->orderBy('jenis')->orderByDesc('source_id')
            ->paginate(10, ['*'], 'pemakaian_page')
            ->appends(array_diff_key($filters, ['pemakaian_page' => true, 'pemakaian_jenis' => true]))->fragment('pemakaian-bs');

        return compact('filters', 'summary', 'modules', 'rows');
    }

    public function sourceResult(User $viewer, string $type, int $id): ?\stdClass
    {
        abort_unless(isset(BsUsageCheckService::TYPES[$type]), 404);
        $model = BsUsageCheckService::TYPES[$type];
        $source = $this->transactions($type, $model, $viewer)->where((new $model)->getTable().'.id', $id);

        return $this->classified($source)->select('usage.*')
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN check_id IS NOT NULL AND needs_recheck = 0 AND keputusan <> 'REVISION' THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE NULL END as selisih")->first();
    }

    public function financeSources(User $viewer): Builder
    {
        abort_unless($viewer->can('akses.approval-finance'), 403);
        $union = null;
        foreach (BsUsageCheckService::TYPES as $type => $model) {
            $part = $this->transactions($type, $model, $viewer, includeDrafts: true);
            $union = $union ? $union->unionAll($part) : $part;
        }

        return $this->classified($union);
    }

    private function classified(Builder $sources): Builder
    {
        // A changed submission waits for a Finance decision on its current version.
        $classified = DB::query()->fromSub($sources, 'usage')->select('usage.*')
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN check_id IS NULL OR needs_recheck = 1 THEN 'PENDING' ELSE keputusan END as finance_status");

        $amounted = DB::query()->fromSub($classified, 'usage')->select('usage.*')
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN finance_status IN ('PENDING', 'REVISION') THEN nominal_user WHEN finance_status = 'APPROVED' THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE 0 END as nominal_pending")
            ->selectRaw("CASE WHEN finance_status IN ('PENDING', 'REVISION') OR (finance_status = 'APPROVED' AND different_items > 0) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as in_pending_group");
        return DB::query()->fromSub($amounted, 'usage');
    }

    private function summary(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        $reviewed = "finance_status = 'APPROVED'";
        return $query->selectRaw("COUNT(*) as confirmed_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(nominal_user), 0) as nominal_user,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'PENDING' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as unchecked_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $reviewed THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as checked_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $reviewed AND different_items > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as different_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(nominal_pending), 0) as nominal_pending,
            COALESCE(SUM(in_pending_group), 0) as pending_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status IN ('PENDING', 'REVISION') THEN nominal_user ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_waiting,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'PENDING' THEN nominal_user ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_unchecked,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'REVISION' THEN nominal_user ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_revision,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'REVISION' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as revision_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'REJECTED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as rejected_count,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN finance_status = 'REJECTED' THEN nominal_user ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_rejected,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $reviewed THEN nominal_finance ELSE 0 END), 0) as nominal_finance,
            COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $reviewed THEN nominal_user - nominal_finance ELSE 0 END), 0) as selisih");
    }

    private function transactions(string $type, string $model, User $viewer, bool $includeDrafts = false): Builder
    {
        $header = (new $model)->getTable();
        $event = BsUsageCheckService::isEvent(new $model);
        $items = $event ? DB::table($header)->select('transaksi_id')
            ->selectRaw('NULL as cost_category_id, ROUND(nominal, 2) as nominal_asal')
            : (new $model)->details()->getRelated()->newQuery()
            ->select('transaksi_id', 'cost_category_id')->selectRaw('ROUND(SUM(nominal), 2) as nominal_asal')
            ->groupBy('transaksi_id', 'cost_category_id')->toBase();
        $amounts = DB::query()->fromSub(clone $items, 'items')->select('transaksi_id')
            ->selectRaw('SUM(nominal_asal) as nominal_user')->groupBy('transaksi_id');
        $latest = BsUsageCheck::query()->where('jenis_transaksi', $type)->latestPerSource()->toBase();
        $approved = DB::table('bs_usage_check_details')->select('bs_usage_check_id')
            ->selectRaw('SUM(nominal_disetujui) as nominal_finance, SUM(CASE WHEN nominal_asal <> nominal_disetujui THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as different_items')
            ->groupBy('bs_usage_check_id');

        $changed = DB::query()->fromSub(clone $items, 'current_item')
            ->leftJoin('bs_usage_check_details as checked_item', function (JoinClause $join) {
                $join->whereRaw('(checked_item.cost_category_id = current_item.cost_category_id OR (checked_item.cost_category_id IS NULL AND current_item.cost_category_id IS NULL))')
                    ->on('checked_item.bs_usage_check_id', '=', 'finance.id');
            })->whereColumn('current_item.transaksi_id', $header.'.transaksi_id')
            ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull('checked_item.id')->orWhereColumn('checked_item.nominal_asal', '<>', 'current_item.nominal_asal'))->selectRaw('1');
        $removed = DB::table('bs_usage_check_details as old_item')
            ->whereColumn('old_item.bs_usage_check_id', 'finance.id')
            ->whereNotExists(DB::query()->fromSub(clone $items, 'remaining_item')
                ->whereColumn('remaining_item.transaksi_id', $header.'.transaksi_id')
                ->whereRaw('(remaining_item.cost_category_id = old_item.cost_category_id OR (remaining_item.cost_category_id IS NULL AND old_item.cost_category_id IS NULL))')->selectRaw('1'))->selectRaw('1');

        if ($event) {
            $amounts = DB::table($header)->select('id as source_id')->selectRaw('ROUND(nominal, 2) as nominal_user');
            $changed = DB::query()->selectRaw('1')->whereNotExists(DB::table('bs_usage_check_details as event_item')
                ->whereColumn('event_item.bs_usage_check_id', 'finance.id')->whereNull('event_item.cost_category_id')
                ->whereRaw('event_item.nominal_asal = ROUND('.$header.'.nominal, 2)')->selectRaw('1'));
            $removed = DB::table('bs_usage_check_details as other_item')->whereColumn('other_item.bs_usage_check_id', 'finance.id')
                ->whereNotNull('other_item.cost_category_id')->selectRaw('1');
        }

        $differences = DB::query()->selectRaw('1')->whereExists($changed)->orWhereExists($removed);
        if (!$event) {
            $lines = (new $model)->details()->getRelated()->newQuery()
                ->select('id', 'transaksi_id', 'cost_category_id', 'remark')
                ->selectRaw('ROUND(nominal, 2) as nominal_asal')->toBase();
            $hasLines = DB::table('bs_usage_check_details as line_check')
                ->whereColumn('line_check.bs_usage_check_id', 'finance.id')->whereNotNull('line_check.source_detail_id')->selectRaw('1');
            $binary = in_array(DB::connection()->getDriverName(), ['mysql', 'mariadb'], true) ? 'BINARY ' : '';
            $changedLines = DB::query()->fromSub(clone $lines, 'source_line')
                ->leftJoin('bs_usage_check_details as checked_line', function (JoinClause $a) {
                    $a->on('checked_line.source_detail_id', '=', 'source_line.id')
                        ->on('checked_line.bs_usage_check_id', '=', 'finance.id');
                })->whereColumn('source_line.transaksi_id', $header.'.transaksi_id')
                ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull('checked_line.id')
                    ->orWhereColumn('checked_line.cost_category_id', '<>', 'source_line.cost_category_id')
                    ->orWhereColumn('checked_line.nominal_asal', '<>', 'source_line.nominal_asal')
                    ->orWhereRaw("{$binary}COALESCE(checked_line.source_remark, '') <> {$binary}COALESCE(source_line.remark, '')"))->selectRaw('1');
            $removedLines = DB::table('bs_usage_check_details as previous_line')
                ->whereColumn('previous_line.bs_usage_check_id', 'finance.id')
                ->whereNotExists(DB::query()->fromSub(clone $lines, 'remaining_line')
                    ->whereColumn('remaining_line.transaksi_id', $header.'.transaksi_id')
                    ->whereColumn('remaining_line.id', 'previous_line.source_detail_id')->selectRaw('1'))->selectRaw('1');
            $legacyDifferences = $differences;
            $differences = DB::query()->selectRaw('1')
                ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereExists($hasLines)
                    ->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereExists($changedLines)->orWhereExists($removedLines)))
                ->orWhere(fn ($q) => $q->whereNotExists($hasLines)->whereExists($legacyDifferences));
        }

        $query = DB::table($header)->join('user as owner', 'owner.id', '=', $header.'.user_id')
            ->leftJoin('user as confirmer', 'confirmer.id', '=', $header.'.validated_by')
            ->leftJoinSub($amounts, 'amounts', $event ? 'amounts.source_id' : 'amounts.transaksi_id', '=', $header.($event ? '.id' : '.transaksi_id'))
            ->leftJoinSub($latest, 'finance', 'finance.transaksi_pemakaian_id', '=', $header.'.id')
            ->leftJoinSub($approved, 'approved', 'approved.bs_usage_check_id', '=', 'finance.id')
            ->select([
                $header.'.id as source_id', $header.'.transaksi_id as no_referensi', $header.'.tanggal',
                $header.'.periode', $header.'.status as source_status', $header.'.user_id', $header.'.validated_at', 'owner.nama as user_name',
                'confirmer.nama as confirmer_name', 'finance.id as check_id', 'finance.document_no as check_no',
                'finance.tanggal_pengecekan', 'finance.created_at as checked_at', 'finance.checker_name',
                'finance.keputusan', 'finance.catatan_keputusan', 'finance.periode as check_period',
                'approved.nominal_finance', 'approved.different_items',
            ])->selectRaw('? as jenis, COALESCE(amounts.nominal_user, 0) as nominal_user', [$type])
            ->selectRaw('CASE WHEN finance.id IS NOT NULL AND (EXISTS ('.$differences->toSql().')'
                .($event ? ' OR finance.source_revision IS NULL OR finance.source_revision <> ' : ' OR COALESCE(finance.source_revision, 0) <> ')
                .$header.'.submission_version) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as needs_recheck', $differences->getBindings());

        if (!$includeDrafts || $event) $query->where($header.'.status', 'VALIDATED');
        if ($includeDrafts && !$event) {
            $query->where(fn ($q) => $q->whereNull($header.'.finance_correction_check_id')->orWhere($header.'.status', 'VALIDATED'));
        }

        if (!$viewer->can('akses.approval-finance') && !$viewer->canSeeAllTransactions()) {
            $query->where($header.'.user_id', $viewer->id);
        }

        return $query;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheck;
use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft;
use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalHeader;
use App\Models\TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader;
use App\Models\PerongkosanEventDalamKota;
use App\Models\PerongkosanEventLuarKota;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\Money;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Encryption\DecryptException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Crypt;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class BsUsageCheckService
{
    public const TYPES = [
        'OPR' => TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class,
        'PD' => TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class,
        'EDK' => PerongkosanEventDalamKota::class,
        'ELK' => PerongkosanEventLuarKota::class,
    ];

    public const LABELS = ['OPR' => 'Operasional Sales', 'PD' => 'Perjalanan Dinas', 'EDK' => 'Event Dalam Kota', 'ELK' => 'Event Luar Kota'];
    public const DECISIONS = ['APPROVED' => 'Dikonfirmasi Finance', 'REJECTED' => 'Ditolak'];

    public static function decisionLabel(string $decision): string
    {
        return self::DECISIONS[$decision] ?? ($decision === 'REVISION' ? 'Perlu Revisi (keputusan lama)' : $decision);
    }

    public static function awaitingResubmission(Model $source): bool
    {
        return $source->status !== 'VALIDATED'
            && (self::isEvent($source) || $source->finance_correction_check_id !== null);
    }

    public static function isEvent(Model $source): bool
    {
        return $source instanceof PerongkosanEventDalamKota || $source instanceof PerongkosanEventLuarKota;
    }

    public function source(string $type, int $id, bool $lock = false): Model
    {
        abort_unless(isset(self::TYPES[$type]), 404);
        $query = self::TYPES[$type]::query();
        if ($lock) {
            $query->lockForUpdate();
        }

        return $query->findOrFail($id);
    }

    public function snapshot(Model $source, bool $lock = false): array
    {
        if (self::isEvent($source)) {
            $type = $source instanceof PerongkosanEventDalamKota ? 'EDK' : 'ELK';
            $items = collect([[
                'source_detail_id' => null, 'source_remark' => $source->description,
                'cost_category_id' => null, 'cost_category_name' => self::LABELS[$type],
                'nominal_asal' => Money::decimal(Money::minor($source->nominal)),
            ]]);
            $fingerprint = hash('sha256', json_encode($source->getAttributes(), JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR));

            return compact('items', 'fingerprint');
        }
        $query = $source->details()->with('costCategory')->orderBy('id');
        if ($lock) {
            $query->lockForUpdate();
        }
        $details = $query->get();
        $items = $details->map(function ($row) {
            $minor = Money::minor($row->nominal);
            if ($minor < 0 || $minor > 999999999999999) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source' => 'Nominal rincian sumber di luar batas yang dapat diperiksa.']);
            }

            return [
                'source_detail_id' => (int) $row->id,
                'source_remark' => $row->remark,
                'source_photo_path' => $row->photo_path,
                'source_photo_name' => $row->photo_name,
                'cost_category_id' => (int) $row->cost_category_id,
                'cost_category_name' => $row->costCategory?->nama ?? 'Kategori #'.$row->cost_category_id,
                'nominal_asal' => Money::decimal($minor),
            ];
        })->values();

        $fingerprint = hash('sha256', json_encode([
            'header' => [$source->getKey(), $source->transaksi_id, $source->user_id, $source->tanggal, $source->status, $source->description, (int) $source->submission_version],
            'details' => $details->map(fn ($row) => [$row->id, $row->cost_category_id, Money::decimal(Money::minor($row->nominal)), $row->remark, ...($row->photo_path ? [$row->photo_path] : [])])->all(),
        ], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR));

        return compact('items', 'fingerprint');
    }

    public function token(string $type, int $id, string $fingerprint): string
    {
        return Crypt::encryptString(json_encode([$type, $id, $fingerprint], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR));
    }

    public function reviewToken(string $type, int $id, ?BsUsageCheck $latest, ?BsUsageCheckDraft $draft): string
    {
        return Crypt::encryptString(json_encode([$type, $id, $latest?->id, $draft?->id, $draft?->version], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR));
    }

    public function store(User $checker, string $type, int $id, array $data): BsUsageCheck|BsUsageCheckDraft
    {
        abort_unless($checker->can('akses.approval-finance'), 403);
        $data['save_action'] = $data['save_action'] ?? 'confirm';
        $explicitDecision = array_key_exists('keputusan', $data);
        if (!$explicitDecision) $data['keputusan'] = 'APPROVED';
        $isDraft = $data['save_action'] === 'draft';
        $data = validator($data, [
            'save_action' => 'required|in:draft,confirm',
            'tanggal_pengecekan' => 'required|date_format:Y-m-d',
            'source_token' => 'required|string|max:4096',
            'review_token' => 'nullable|required_if:save_action,draft|string|max:4096',
            'keputusan' => 'required|in:APPROVED,REJECTED',
            'catatan_keputusan' => 'nullable|'.($isDraft ? '' : 'required_if:keputusan,REJECTED|').'string|max:2000',
            'details' => 'required|array|min:1|max:1000',
            'details.*' => 'required|array:source_detail_id,cost_category_id,nominal_disetujui,catatan',
            'details.*.source_detail_id' => 'present|nullable|integer|min:1|distinct',
            'details.*.cost_category_id' => 'present|nullable|integer',
            'details.*.nominal_disetujui' => [$isDraft ? 'present' : 'required', 'nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0', 'max:9999999999999.99', 'regex:/^\d{1,13}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/D'],
            'details.*.catatan' => 'nullable|string|max:2000',
        ])->validate();
        try {
            $expected = json_decode(Crypt::decryptString($data['source_token']), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        } catch (DecryptException|\JsonException $exception) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source_token' => 'Referensi pengecekan tidak valid. Buka kembali form dari daftar transaksi.']);
        }

        $reference = $this->source($type, $id);
        $posting = app(FinanceBsExpenseService::class);
        $period = $posting->period($reference);
        $owner = $posting->owner($reference);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($checker, $type, $id, $data, $expected, $isDraft, $explicitDecision, $reference, $posting, $period, $owner) {
            // Both drafts and confirmations must respect a frozen accounting period.
            app(PeriodService::class)->lockOpen($period);
            if (!$isDraft) {
                // Match transaction writes and period closing: period, owners, then source.
                foreach (collect([$owner, (int) $reference->user_id])->filter()->unique()->sort() as $userId) {
                    DB::table('user')->where('id', $userId)->lockForUpdate()->first();
                }
            }
            $source = $this->source($type, $id, true);
            if ($posting->period($source) !== $period || $posting->owner($source) !== $owner
                || (int) $source->user_id !== (int) $reference->user_id) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source' => 'Transaksi berubah. Muat ulang pemeriksaan Finance.']);
            }
            $latest = BsUsageCheck::where('jenis_transaksi', $type)->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $id)->latest('id')->first();
            $draft = BsUsageCheckDraft::where('jenis_transaksi', $type)->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $id)->lockForUpdate()->first();
            if ($draft && !isset(self::DECISIONS[$draft->keputusan]) && !$explicitDecision) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['keputusan' => 'Pilih Dikonfirmasi Finance atau Ditolak untuk melanjutkan draft lama.']);
            }
            // Source writes are serialized; versioning also catches stale browser tabs.
            $context = null;
            if (!empty($data['review_token'])) {
                try {
                    $context = json_decode(Crypt::decryptString($data['review_token']), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
                } catch (DecryptException|\JsonException $exception) {
                    throw ValidationException::withMessages(['review_token' => 'Form pemeriksaan tidak valid. Buka kembali pemeriksaan dari daftar Finance.']);
                }
            }
            if ((!empty($data['review_token']) && $context !== [$type, $id, $latest?->id, $draft?->id, $draft?->version])
                || ($draft && empty($data['review_token']))) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['review_token' => 'Pemeriksaan sudah diperbarui atau dikonfirmasi. Muat ulang untuk melanjutkan versi terbaru.']);
            }
            if ((!$isDraft || self::isEvent($source)) && $source->status !== 'VALIDATED') {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source' => 'Transaksi harus dikonfirmasi oleh pengguna dan disetujui pemilik saldo bila diperlukan sebelum dikonfirmasi Finance.']);
            }
            if (self::awaitingResubmission($source)) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source' => 'Perbaikan pengajuan harus dikonfirmasi kembali sebelum diperiksa Finance.']);
            }
            $snapshot = $this->snapshot($source, true);
            if ($expected !== [$type, $id, $snapshot['fingerprint']]) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['source_token' => 'Transaksi sumber berubah. Muat ulang form dan periksa kembali rincian sebelum menyimpan.']);
            }
            $items = $snapshot['items'];
            $submitted = collect($data['details']);
            $expectedIds = $items->pluck('source_detail_id')->map(fn ($value) => (int) $value)->sort()->values()->all();
            $submittedIds = $submitted->pluck('source_detail_id')->map(fn ($value) => (int) $value)->sort()->values()->all();
            if ($items->isEmpty() || $expectedIds !== $submittedIds) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['details' => 'Rincian harus memuat setiap baris biaya sumber tepat satu kali. Muat ulang form pemeriksaan.']);
            }

            $source->load('user');
            $attributes = [
                'tanggal_pengecekan' => $data['tanggal_pengecekan'],
                'jenis_transaksi' => $type,
                'transaksi_pemakaian_id' => $source->getKey(),
                'no_referensi' => $source->transaksi_id ?: $type.' #'.$source->getKey(),
                'user_id' => $source->user_id,
                'user_checker_id' => $checker->id,
                'user_name' => $source->user->nama,
                'checker_name' => $checker->nama,
                'periode' => app(PeriodService::class)->ofDate($data['tanggal_pengecekan']),
                'source_fingerprint' => $snapshot['fingerprint'],
                'keputusan' => $data['keputusan'],
                'catatan_keputusan' => $data['catatan_keputusan'] ?? null,
                'revision_of_id' => $latest?->id,
            ];
            $submitted = $submitted->keyBy(fn ($input) => (int) $input['source_detail_id']);
            $details = $items->map(function ($item) use ($submitted, $data) {
                $input = $submitted->get((int) $item['source_detail_id']);
                if ((int) $input['cost_category_id'] !== (int) $item['cost_category_id']) {
                    throw ValidationException::withMessages(['details' => 'Kategori rincian tidak sesuai dengan sumber. Muat ulang form pemeriksaan.']);
                }
                return $item + [
                    'nominal_disetujui' => $data['keputusan'] === 'REJECTED' ? '0.00'
                        : ($input['nominal_disetujui'] === null ? null : Money::decimal(Money::minor($input['nominal_disetujui']))),
                    'catatan' => $input['catatan'] ?? null,
                ];
            });
            if ($isDraft) {
                $draft ??= new BsUsageCheckDraft();
                $draft->fill($attributes + ['details' => $details->all(), 'version' => ($draft->version ?? 0) + 1])->save();

                return $draft;
            }
            if ($owner !== null && $data['keputusan'] === 'APPROVED') {
                $cash = app(BsCashFlowService::class);
                $available = Money::minor($cash->availableBalance($cash->calculate($owner, $period)));
                $previous = $posting->approvedAmount($source);
                $approved = $details->sum(fn ($item) => Money::minor($item['nominal_disetujui']));
                if ($approved > $previous && $approved > $available + $previous) {
                    throw ValidationException::withMessages(['bs_balance' => 'Nominal konfirmasi Finance melebihi saldo BS yang tersedia. Sisa untuk transaksi ini: Rp '.number_format(($available + $previous) / 100, 2, ',', '.').'.']);
                }
            }
            $check = BsUsageCheck::create($attributes + [
                'document_no' => app(DocumentNumberService::class)->next('CEKBS', $data['tanggal_pengecekan']),
                'source_revision' => (int) $source->submission_version,
            ]);
            $check->details()->createMany($details->all());
            // The draft is consumed only after the complete immutable document was saved.
            $draft?->delete();
            if ($owner !== null) {
                app(BsCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot($owner, $period);
                app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot($owner, $period);
            }

            return $check;
        }, 3);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

class BsUsageCheckFormService
{
    public function __construct(private BsUsageCheckService $service) {}

    public function data(Model $transaction, string $jenis): array
    {
        abort_unless(app(PeriodService::class)->isOpen(app(FinanceBsExpenseService::class)->period($transaction)),
            403, 'Periode transaksi sudah ditutup. Pemeriksaan Finance dikunci.');
        abort_unless($transaction->status === 'VALIDATED', 403,
            'Transaksi belum dikonfirmasi oleh pemohon. Pemeriksaan Finance hanya dapat dilakukan pada transaksi yang sudah divalidasi.');
        $service = $this->service;
        $source = (int) $transaction->id;
        $transaction->load('user');
        $snapshot = $service->snapshot($transaction);
        $draft = BsUsageCheckDraft::where('jenis_transaksi', $jenis)->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $source)->first();
        $revisionOf = $transaction->financeChecks()->latest('id')->with('details')->first();
        $previous = $draft ? collect($draft->details) : ($revisionOf?->details->map(fn ($detail) => $detail->getAttributes()) ?? collect());
        if (!$draft && $revisionOf?->keputusan === 'REJECTED') $previous = collect();
        $previousLines = $previous->filter(fn ($item) => !empty($item['source_detail_id']))->keyBy('source_detail_id');
        $previousCategories = $previous->filter(fn ($item) => empty($item['source_detail_id']))
            ->keyBy(fn ($item) => (int) $item['cost_category_id']);
        $categoryCounts = $snapshot['items']->countBy(fn ($item) => (int) $item['cost_category_id']);
        $needsItemReview = false;
        $items = $snapshot['items']->map(function ($item) use ($previousLines, $previousCategories, $categoryCounts, &$needsItemReview) {
            $saved = $previousLines->get($item['source_detail_id']);
            $legacy = $previousCategories->get((int) $item['cost_category_id']);
            $ambiguous = !$saved && $legacy && $categoryCounts[(int) $item['cost_category_id']] > 1;
            $needsItemReview = $needsItemReview || $ambiguous;
            if (!$saved && !$ambiguous) $saved = $legacy;
            $same = $saved && (int) $saved['cost_category_id'] === (int) $item['cost_category_id']
                && \App\Support\Money::minor($saved['nominal_asal']) === \App\Support\Money::minor($item['nominal_asal'])
                && (empty($saved['source_detail_id']) || ($saved['source_remark'] ?? '') === ($item['source_remark'] ?? ''))
                && ($saved['source_photo_path'] ?? null) === ($item['source_photo_path'] ?? null);
            return $item + [
                'nominal_disetujui' => $ambiguous ? null : ($same ? $saved['nominal_disetujui'] : $item['nominal_asal']),
                'catatan' => $same ? ($saved['catatan'] ?? '') : '',
            ];
        });
        $previousFingerprint = $draft?->source_fingerprint ?? $revisionOf?->source_fingerprint;
        $sourceChanged = $previousFingerprint && $previousFingerprint !== $snapshot['fingerprint'];
        $defaults = [
            'tanggal_pengecekan' => $draft?->tanggal_pengecekan->toDateString() ?? \App\Support\AppTime::now()->toDateString(),
            'keputusan' => $draft && !isset(BsUsageCheckService::DECISIONS[$draft->keputusan]) ? '' : ($draft?->keputusan ?? 'APPROVED'),
            'catatan_keputusan' => $draft?->catatan_keputusan ?? '',
        ];

        return [
            'transaction' => $transaction, 'jenis' => $jenis, 'items' => $items,
            'sourceToken' => $service->token($jenis, $source, $snapshot['fingerprint']),
            'reviewToken' => $service->reviewToken($jenis, $source, $revisionOf, $draft),
            'draft' => $draft, 'revisionOf' => $revisionOf, 'defaults' => $defaults, 'sourceChanged' => $sourceChanged,
            'needsItemReview' => $needsItemReview,
            'historyCount' => $transaction->financeChecks()->count(),
        ];
    }
}

BEGIN FILE 052: app/Services/PeriodService.php
SHA-256: d5baf731e6492d374ae3061b1c7f2c5aceadf1691cc1161328dadfbe8adf4d5d
Ukuran sumber: 3130 byte
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Periode;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class PeriodService
{
    public function normalize(string $periode): string
    {
        if (!preg_match('/^(\d{4})-?(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/D', $periode, $parts)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['periode' => 'Periode harus berupa YYYYMM atau YYYY-MM dengan bulan yang valid.']);
        }
        return $parts[1].'-'.$parts[2];
    }

    public function key(string $periode): string
    {
        return str_replace('-', '', $this->normalize($periode));
    }

    public function previous(string $periode): string
    {
        return \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::createFromFormat('!Y-m', $this->normalize($periode))->subMonth()->format('Y-m');
    }

    public function next(string $periode): string
    {
        return \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::createFromFormat('!Y-m', $this->normalize($periode))->addMonth()->format('Y-m');
    }

    /** Caller must hold a database transaction until its write has completed. */
    public function lockOpen(string $periode): Periode
    {
        $periode = $this->normalize($periode);
        $record = Periode::where('periode', $periode)->lockForUpdate()->first();
        if (!$record) {
            if ($periode < $this->current()) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['tanggal' => "Periode {$periode} belum tersedia atau sudah ditutup."]);
            }
            $record = Periode::create(['periode' => $periode, 'status' => 'OPEN']);
        }
        if ($record->status !== 'OPEN') {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['tanggal' => "Periode {$periode} sudah ditutup."]);
        }
        return $record;
    }
    /** Bulan riil saat ini, format YYYY-MM */
    public function current(): string
    {
        return \App\Support\AppTime::now()->format('Y-m');
    }

    /** Turunkan periode dari tanggal transaksi (business rule rev4) */
    public function ofDate(string $tanggal): string
    {
        return substr($tanggal, 0, 7);
    }

    /**
     * Status sebuah periode.
     * Jika barisnya belum ada (auto-roll belum menyentuh):
     *  - bulan lampau      => dianggap CLOSED (mencegah input ke bulan liar)
     *  - bulan ini / depan => dianggap OPEN
     */
    public function statusOf(string $periode): string
    {
        $periode = $this->normalize($periode);
        $row = Periode::where('periode', $periode)->first();

        return $row ? $row->status : ($periode < $this->current() ? 'CLOSED' : 'OPEN');
    }

    public function isOpen(string $periode): bool
    {
        return $this->statusOf($periode) === 'OPEN';
    }

    /** 🎯 GATE BACKEND: lempar error validasi jika periode tertutup */
    public function assertOpen(string $periode): void
    {
        if (! $this->isOpen($periode)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'tanggal' => "Periode {$periode} sudah DITUTUP (tutup buku). Transaksi tidak dapat disimpan — pilih tanggal pada periode yang masih OPEN.",
            ]);
        }
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Periode;

class BonSementaraService
{
    /**
     * Periode berjalan mengikuti bulan kalender aplikasi.
     * Buat bulan yang belum tersedia tanpa membuka kembali periode CLOSED.
     */
    public function getActivePeriode(): string
    {
        $currentMonth = app(PeriodService::class)->current();

        Periode::firstOrCreate(
            ['periode' => $currentMonth],
            ['status' => 'OPEN']
        );

        return $currentMonth;
    }

    public function getSaldoAwal($userId, $periode)
    {
        return app(BsCashFlowService::class)->calculate((int) $userId, $periode)['saldo_awal'];
    }

    public function getRealtimeSisaSaldo($userId, $periode)
    {
        $service = app(BsCashFlowService::class);
        return $service->availableBalance($service->calculate((int) $userId, $periode));
    }

}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\PengajuanBs;
use App\Services\BsAttachmentService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class PengajuanBsAttachmentController extends Controller
{
    public function __invoke(Request $request, PengajuanBs $bs)
    {
        $user = $request->user();
        $canViewSubmission = $user->can('akses.pengajuan-bs') && (
            $user->canSeeAllTransactions()
            || (int) $bs->user_id === (int) $user->id
            || $bs->members()->whereKey($user->id)->exists()
        );
        $isApprover = (int) $bs->atasan_user_id === (int) $user->id && $user->can('approval-atasan');
        abort_unless($canViewSubmission || $isApprover || $user->can('akses.approval-finance'), 403);

        $path = $bs->attachment_path;
        abort_unless($bs->isPerjalananDinas() && is_string($path)
            && preg_match('~\Apengajuan_bs_attachments/[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\.(pdf|jpe?g)\z~', $path), 404);
        $disk = Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        abort_unless($disk->exists($path), 404, 'Lampiran tidak ditemukan.');

        return $disk->response($path, $bs->attachment_name ?: basename($path), [
            'Content-Type' => str_ends_with($path, '.pdf') ? 'application/pdf' : 'image/jpeg',
            'X-Content-Type-Options' => 'nosniff',
            'Cache-Control' => 'private, no-store',
        ], $request->boolean('download') ? 'attachment' : 'inline');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDetail;
use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalDetail;
use App\Models\TransaksiPerjalananDinasDetail;
use App\Services\TransactionDetailPhotoService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class TransactionDetailPhotoController extends Controller
{
    public function show(Request $request, string $type, int $detail)
    {
        $model = match ($type) {
            'OPR' => TransaksiOperasionalDetail::class,
            'PD' => TransaksiPerjalananDinasDetail::class,
            default => abort(404),
        };
        $row = $model::with('header')->findOrFail($detail);
        abort_unless($row->header, 404);
        abort_unless($request->user()->canViewBsTransaction($row->header)
            || $request->user()->can('akses.approval-finance'), 403);

        return $this->respond($request, $row->photo_path, $row->photo_name);
    }

    public function history(Request $request, BsUsageCheckDetail $detail)
    {
        // Uses the same Finance permission as the immutable check detail page.
        abort_unless($request->user()->can('akses.approval-finance'), 403);

        return $this->respond($request, $detail->source_photo_path, $detail->source_photo_name);
    }

    private function respond(Request $request, ?string $path, ?string $name)
    {
        abort_unless($path && preg_match('~\Areceipts/[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\.(jpe?g|png|webp)\z~', $path), 404);
        $disk = Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK);
        abort_unless($disk->exists($path), 404, 'Foto tidak ditemukan.');
        $mime = match (pathinfo($path, PATHINFO_EXTENSION)) {
            'png' => 'image/png', 'webp' => 'image/webp', default => 'image/jpeg',
        };

        return $disk->response($path, $name ?: basename($path), [
            'Content-Type' => $mime, 'X-Content-Type-Options' => 'nosniff', 'Cache-Control' => 'private, no-store',
        ], $request->boolean('download') ? 'attachment' : 'inline');
    }
}

                </tbody><tfoot><tr class="fw-bold"><td colspan="3">Total</td><td data-label="Nominal asal (Rp)" class="check-money" id="check-total-original">{{ number_format(\App\Support\Money::decimal($items->sum(fn ($item) => \App\Support\Money::minor($item['nominal_asal']))), 2, ',', '.') }}</td><td data-label="Total Disetujui (Rp)" class="check-money" id="check-total-approved">—</td><td data-label="Total Selisih (Rp)" class="check-money" id="check-total-difference">—</td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table></div>

                modalImage.src = imageUrl;

BEGIN FILE 065: database/seeders/PermissionSeeder.php
SHA-256: e9c3fe10a44041926f2cb8dede723646ea4742a379540d4944be42109f12fea2
Ukuran sumber: 4522 byte

<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Models\Role as MasterRole;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role as SpatieRole;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class PermissionSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

        // ==========================================
        // 1. DAFTAR RESMI PERMISSION (format dokumen rev4)
        // ==========================================
        $canonical = [
            'akses.pengajuan-bs',
           
            'akses.approval-finance',
            'akses.transaksi-operasional',
            'akses.transaksi-perjalanan-dinas',
            'akses.event-dalam-kota',
            'akses.event-luar-kota',
            'akses.laporan-bs',
             'akses.settle-bs',
            'akses.cash-flow',
            'akses.master-data',
            'akses.access-management',
        ];

        foreach ($canonical as $name) {
            Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => $name, 'guard_name' => 'web']);
        }

        // ==========================================
        // 2. 🧹 CLEANUP: HAPUS PERMISSION LAMA DI LUAR DAFTAR RESMI
        //    (mis. sisa format lama: akses.transaksi.operasional,
        //     akses.approval.atasan, akses.master.data, dll)
        //    Pivot di role_has_permissions & model_has_permissions
        //    ikut terhapus otomatis karena ON DELETE CASCADE.
        // ==========================================
        Permission::whereNotIn('name', $canonical)->get()->each->delete();

        // ==========================================
        // 3. CERMINKAN MASTER ROLES (User Right Group) KE SPATIE ROLES
        // ==========================================
        $masterCodes = MasterRole::pluck('code')->filter()->values();

        foreach ($masterCodes as $code) {
            SpatieRole::firstOrCreate(['name' => $code, 'guard_name' => 'web']);
        }

        if ($masterCodes->isNotEmpty()) {
            SpatieRole::whereNotIn('name', $masterCodes)->get()->each->delete();
        }

        // ==========================================
        // 4. MATRIKS DEFAULT (hanya jika role masih kosong)
        // ==========================================
        $all = Permission::pluck('name')->toArray();

        $matrix = [
            'SALES' => [
                'akses.pengajuan-bs',
                'akses.transaksi-operasional',
                'akses.transaksi-perjalanan-dinas',
            ],
            'MARKETING' => [
                'akses.pengajuan-bs',
                'akses.event-dalam-kota',
                'akses.event-luar-kota',
            ],
            'OFFICE' => [
                'akses.pengajuan-bs',
                'akses.transaksi-operasional',
                'akses.laporan-bs',
            ],
            'FINANCE' => [
                'akses.approval-finance',
                'akses.laporan-bs',
                'akses.settle-bs', 
                'akses.cash-flow',
                
            ],
            'DIRECTOR' => [
                'akses.laporan-bs',
                'akses.settle-bs', 
                'akses.cash-flow',
            ],
            'DIR' => [
                'akses.laporan-bs',
                'akses.cash-flow',
            ],
            'HR' => [
                'akses.master-data',
            ],
            'ADMINISTRATOR' => $all,
            'ADMIN' => $all,
        ];

        foreach ($matrix as $code => $perms) {
            $role = SpatieRole::where('name', $code)->first();
            if ($role && $role->permissions()->count() === 0) {
                $role->syncPermissions($perms);
            }
        }

        // ==========================================
        // 5. SYNC SEMUA USER KE ROLE MASTER
        // ==========================================
        User::with('role')->get()->each(function (User $user) {
            $code = $user->role?->code;

            if (!$code) {
                $user->syncRoles([]);
                return;
            }

            $spatieRole = SpatieRole::firstOrCreate(['name' => $code, 'guard_name' => 'web']);
            $user->syncRoles([$spatieRole]);
        });

        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}

BEGIN FILE 067: tests/Concerns/TransactionDetailPhotoScenarios.php
SHA-256: 63d9ec8588d4d0f7db41516071e5ba7eb2f7ecb6f5edd7e1d42ebbadf8b0d3c2
Ukuran sumber: 13717 byte
