<?php

namespace Tests\Concerns;

use App\Models\TransaksiOperasionalHeader;
use App\Models\TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\BsUsageCheckService;
use App\Services\TransactionDetailPhotoService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\DataProvider;

trait TransactionDetailPhotoScenarios
{
    public static function photoModules(): array
    {
        return ['Operasional' => [false], 'Perjalanan Dinas' => [true]];
    }

    private function photoModule(bool $travel): string
    {
        return $travel ? 'transaksi_perjalanan_dinas' : 'transaksi_operasional';
    }

    private function photoKey($source)
    {
        return $source instanceof TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader ? $source->transaksi_id : $source->id;
    }

    private function photoSource(bool $travel)
    {
        Storage::fake(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK);
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $payload['details'][0]['photo'] = UploadedFile::fake()->image('struk.jpg', 4, 4);
        $this->post(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.store'), $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $model = $travel ? TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class : TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class;

        return $model::latest('id')->firstOrFail();
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_create_keeps_sparse_rows_and_private_access(bool $travel): void
    {
        Storage::fake(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK);
        Storage::fake('public');
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $line = $payload['details'][0];
        $payload['details'] = [
            2 => array_replace($line, ['remark' => 'Tiket', 'photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('tiket.jpg', 4, 4)]),
            8 => array_replace($line, ['remark' => 'Makan', 'photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('makan.png', 5, 5)]),
            11 => array_replace($line, ['remark' => 'Tanpa foto', 'photo_path' => 'receipts/forged.jpg']),
        ];
        $this->post(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.store'), $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $model = $travel ? TransaksiPerjalananDinasHeader::class : TransaksiOperasionalHeader::class;
        $source = $model::firstOrFail();
        $rows = $source->details()->orderBy('id')->get();
        $this->assertCount(3, $rows);
        $this->assertSame('tiket.jpg', $rows[0]->photo_name);
        $this->assertSame('makan.png', $rows[1]->photo_name);
        $this->assertNull($rows[2]->photo_path);
        $this->assertCount(2, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk('public')->allFiles());
        $this->get($rows[0]->photo_url)->assertOk()->assertHeader('Content-Type', 'image/jpeg')->assertHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
        $response = $this->get($rows[1]->photo_url)->assertOk()->assertHeader('Content-Type', 'image/png');
        $this->assertSame(Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->get($rows[1]->photo_path), $response->streamedContent());
        $this->assertStringContainsString('no-store', $response->headers->get('Cache-Control'));
        $this->get($rows[0]->photo_url.'?download=1')->assertDownload('tiket.jpg');
        $this->get(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.show', $this->photoKey($source)))->assertOk()->assertSee($rows[0]->photo_url);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(3))->get($rows[0]->photo_url)->assertForbidden();
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2))->get($rows[0]->photo_url)->assertOk();
        auth()->logout();
        $this->get($rows[0]->photo_url)->assertRedirect(route('login'));
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_edit_preserves_replaces_adds_and_removes(bool $travel): void
    {
        $source = $this->photoSource($travel);
        $row = $source->details()->firstOrFail();
        $oldPath = $row->photo_path;
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $line = $payload['details'][0];
        $payload['details'] = [$row->id => $line];
        $url = route($this->photoModule($travel).'.update', $this->photoKey($source));
        $this->put($url, $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $this->assertSame($oldPath, $row->fresh()->photo_path);
        $payload['details'][$row->id]['photo'] = UploadedFile::fake()->image('pengganti.png', 5, 5);
        $payload['new_details'][9] = $line + ['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('parkir.jpg', 6, 6)];
        $this->put($url, $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $row->refresh();
        $this->assertSame('pengganti.png', $row->photo_name);
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertMissing($oldPath);
        $this->assertSame('parkir.jpg', $source->details()->latest('id')->first()->photo_name);
        $this->get(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.show', $this->photoKey($source)).'?edit=1')->assertOk()
            ->assertSee('enctype="multipart/form-data"', false)->assertSee($row->photo_url)
            ->assertSee('name="details['.$row->id.'][remove_photo]"', false);
        unset($payload['new_details'], $payload['details'][$row->id]['photo']);
        $payload['details'][$row->id]['remove_photo'] = 1;
        $path = $row->photo_path;
        $this->put($url, $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $this->assertNull($row->fresh()->photo_path);
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertMissing($path);
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_add_endpoint_and_deletion_cleanup(bool $travel): void
    {
        $source = $this->photoSource($travel);
        $row = $source->details()->firstOrFail();
        $prefix = $this->photoModule($travel);
        $key = $this->photoKey($source);
        $line = $this->transactionPayload($travel)['details'][0];
        $this->post(route($prefix.'.store_detail', $key), ['details' => [7 => $line + ['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('tambahan.jpg', 3, 3)]]])->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $new = $source->details()->latest('id')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('tambahan.jpg', $new->photo_name);
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        unset($payload['details']);
        $payload['delete_details'] = [$row->id];
        $this->put(route($prefix.'.update', $key), $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertMissing($row->photo_path);
        $this->get($row->photo_url)->assertNotFound();
        $this->delete(route($prefix.'.destroy', $key))->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertMissing($new->photo_path);
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_rejects_invalid_uploads_without_saving(bool $travel): void
    {
        Storage::fake(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK);
        $text = UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('fake.jpg', 'not an image');
        $files = [
            new UploadedFile($text->getRealPath(), 'fake.jpg', 'image/jpeg', null, true),
            UploadedFile::fake()->image('oversize.jpg')->size(2049),
            UploadedFile::fake()->image('wrong.gif'),
            UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('vector.svg', '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></svg>'),
        ];
        foreach ($files as $file) {
            $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
            $payload['details'][0]['photo'] = $file;
            $this->post(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.store'), $payload)->assertSessionHasErrors('details.0.photo');
        }
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
        $this->assertDatabaseCount($travel ? 'transaksi_perjalanan_dinas_header' : 'transaksi_operasional_area_sales_header', 0);
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_cannot_change_another_transactions_item_or_finalized_source(bool $travel): void
    {
        $source = $this->photoSource($travel);
        $other = $this->expense($this->bs(), travel: $travel, attributes: ['user_id' => 3]);
        $row = $other->details()->firstOrFail();
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $payload['details'] = [$row->id => $payload['details'][0] + ['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('foreign.jpg')]];
        $this->put(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.update', $this->photoKey($source)), $payload)->assertSessionHasErrors('details');
        $this->assertNull($row->fresh()->photo_path);
        $this->assertCount(1, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
        $row = $source->details()->first();
        $source->update(['status' => 'VALIDATED']);
        $payload['details'] = [$row->id => $this->transactionPayload($travel)['details'][0] + ['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('final.jpg')]];
        $this->putJson(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.update', $this->photoKey($source)), $payload)->assertUnprocessable();
        $this->assertSame($row->photo_path, $row->fresh()->photo_path);
        $this->assertCount(1, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_failed_confirmation_cleans_up_upload(bool $travel): void
    {
        Storage::fake(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK);
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $payload['save_action'] = 'confirm';
        $payload['details'][0]['photo'] = UploadedFile::fake()->image('gagal.jpg');
        // No BS balance exists, so confirmation rolls back after the file was stored.
        $this->post(route($this->photoModule($travel).'.store'), $payload)->assertSessionHasErrors('bs_balance');
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
        $this->assertDatabaseCount($travel ? 'transaksi_perjalanan_dinas_header' : 'transaksi_operasional_area_sales_header', 0);
    }

    public function test_detail_photo_outer_rollback_preserves_old_file_and_discards_replacement(): void
    {
        $source = $this->photoSource(false);
        $row = $source->details()->first();
        Route::post('/test-photo-rollback', function (Request $request) use ($row) {
            $row->update(app(TransactionDetailPhotoService::class)->attributes($request, 'details.0'));
            return response('Failure after upload', 500);
        })->middleware(['web', 'auth', 'bs.atomic'])->name('test-photo.rollback');
        $this->post('/test-photo-rollback', ['tanggal' => '2026-09-16', 'details' => [['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('replacement.jpg')]]])->assertStatus(500);
        $this->assertSame('struk.jpg', $row->fresh()->photo_name);
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertExists($row->fresh()->photo_path);
        $this->assertCount(1, Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    #[DataProvider('photoModules')]
    public function test_detail_photo_finance_history_keeps_original_evidence_after_correction(bool $travel): void
    {
        $this->bs();
        $source = $this->photoSource($travel);
        $row = $source->details()->firstOrFail();
        $oldPath = $row->photo_path;
        $type = $travel ? 'PD' : 'OPR';
        $prefix = $this->photoModule($travel);
        $this->post(route($prefix.'.validate', $this->photoKey($source)))->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $source->refresh();
        $check = $this->recapDecision($source, $type, 'REJECTED', '0', 'Perbaiki foto struk');
        $saved = $check->details()->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame($oldPath, $saved->source_photo_path);
        $historyUrl = route('transaction-detail-photo.history', $saved->id);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2))->get($historyUrl)->assertOk();
        $this->get(route('bs-usage-checks.show', $check))->assertOk()->assertSee($historyUrl);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(1));
        $this->get($historyUrl)->assertForbidden();
        $this->post(route('transaction-recap.correct', [$type, $source->id]), ['check_id' => $check->id])->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $before = app(BsUsageCheckService::class)->snapshot($source->fresh())['fingerprint'];
        $payload = $this->transactionPayload($travel);
        $payload['details'] = [$row->id => $payload['details'][0] + ['photo' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('koreksi.png')]];
        $this->put(route($prefix.'.update', $this->photoKey($source)), $payload)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $this->assertNotSame($before, app(BsUsageCheckService::class)->snapshot($source->fresh())['fingerprint']);
        $this->assertSame($oldPath, $saved->fresh()->source_photo_path);
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->assertExists($oldPath);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2))->get($historyUrl)->assertOk();
    }

    public function test_detail_photo_missing_file_and_invalid_path_return_not_found(): void
    {
        $source = $this->photoSource(false);
        $row = $source->details()->firstOrFail();
        $url = $row->photo_url;
        Storage::disk(TransactionDetailPhotoService::DISK)->delete($row->photo_path);
        $this->get($url)->assertNotFound();
        DB::table($row->getTable())->where('id', $row->id)->update(['photo_path' => '../secret.jpg']);
        $this->get($url)->assertNotFound();
    }
}

<?php

namespace Tests\Concerns;

trait BsAttachmentScenarios
{
    private function attachmentPayload(?UploadedFile $file = null): array
    {
        return ['tipe_pengajuan' => 'perjalanan_dinas', 'nominal_pengajuan' => '100000',
            'description' => 'Perjalanan uji', 'attachment' => $file];
    }

    private function travelPdf(): UploadedFile
    {
        return UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('Izin perjalanan.pdf', "%PDF-1.4\n1 0 obj\n<<>>\nendobj\n%%EOF");
    }

    private function submitAttachment(): PengajuanBs
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        Storage::fake('public');
        $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))
            ->assertRedirect(route('pengajuan-bs.index'))->assertSessionHasNoErrors();

        return PengajuanBs::latest('id')->firstOrFail();
    }

    public function test_attachment_pdf_is_private_and_survives_the_outer_commit(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->submitAttachment();
        $this->assertSame('Izin perjalanan.pdf', $bs->attachment_name);
        $this->assertSame('PENDING_ATASAN', $bs->status);
        Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->assertExists($bs->attachment_path);
        $this->assertCount(1, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk('public')->allFiles());
        $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->assertOk()
            ->assertHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf')
            ->assertHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
        $this->assertStringStartsWith('%PDF-1.4', $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->streamedContent());
        $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', ['bs' => $bs, 'download' => 1]))
            ->assertDownload('Izin perjalanan.pdf');
        $this->assertStringContainsString('no-store', $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->headers->get('Cache-Control'));
    }

    public function test_attachment_jpeg_can_be_uploaded(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload(
            UploadedFile::fake()->image('izin.jpg', 2, 2)
        ))->assertRedirect(route('pengajuan-bs.index'))->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $bs = PengajuanBs::firstOrFail();
        $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->assertOk()->assertHeader('Content-Type', 'image/jpeg');
    }

    public static function invalidAttachments(): array
    {
        return ['missing' => ['missing'], 'spoofed PDF' => ['spoofed'], 'PNG' => ['png'],
            'too large' => ['large'], 'wrong extension' => ['extension']];
    }

    #[DataProvider('invalidAttachments')]
    public function test_attachment_validation_rejects_invalid_uploads(string $case): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        $spoofed = UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('izin.pdf', 'This is plain text, not a PDF.');
        $file = match ($case) {
            'missing' => null,
            // Real UploadedFile detects the content MIME; the client's PDF label is untrusted.
            'spoofed' => new UploadedFile($spoofed->getRealPath(), 'izin.pdf', 'application/pdf', null, true),
            'png' => UploadedFile::fake()->image('izin.png', 2, 2),
            'large' => $this->travelPdf()->size(2049),
            'extension' => UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('izin.txt', "%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF"),
        };
        $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload($file))->assertSessionHasErrors('attachment');
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 0);
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public function test_attachment_is_ignored_for_operational_submission(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        $data = $this->attachmentPayload(UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('invalid.txt', 'test'));
        $data['tipe_pengajuan'] = 'operasional_sales';
        $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $data)->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
        $this->assertNull(PengajuanBs::firstOrFail()->attachment_path);
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public function test_attachment_authorizes_each_reader_and_denies_guests_and_unrelated_users(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->submitAttachment();
        $url = route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(3))->get($url)->assertForbidden();
        $bs->members()->attach(3);
        $this->get($url)->assertOk();

        $supervisor = User::findOrFail(2);
        $supervisor->syncPermissions([]);
        $this->actingAs($supervisor)->get($url)->assertOk();
        DB::table('user')->insert(['id' => 4, 'nama' => 'Finance uji', 'is_active' => 1]);
        $finance = User::findOrFail(4);
        $finance->givePermissionTo('akses.approval-finance');
        $this->actingAs($finance)->get($url)->assertOk();
        $finance->syncPermissions([]);
        $this->get($url)->assertForbidden();

        $bs->update(['atasan_user_id' => null]);
        $this->actingAs($supervisor)->get($url)->assertForbidden();
        auth()->logout();
        $this->get($url)->assertRedirect(route('login'));
    }

    public function test_attachment_is_visible_to_applicant_supervisor_and_finance(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->submitAttachment();
        $url = route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs);
        $download = route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', ['bs' => $bs, 'download' => 1]);
        $assertPanel = function ($response) use ($url, $download) {
            $response->assertOk()->assertSee('Izin perjalanan.pdf')
                ->assertSee('Buka PDF')->assertSee('Unduh')->assertSee('Pratinjau PDF')
                ->assertSee($download)->assertSee('<iframe src="'.$url.'"', false)
                ->assertSee('title="Pratinjau PDF: Izin perjalanan.pdf"', false);
        };
        $assertPanel($this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.show', $bs)));
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $assertPanel($this->get(route('approval-atasan.detail', $bs)));
        $assertPanel($this->get(route('approval-finance.detail', $bs)));
    }

    public function test_attachment_jpeg_has_open_and_download_links_without_pdf_preview(): void

    }

    {

        $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->assertNotFound();
        $bs->update(['attachment_path' => null]);
        $this->get(route('pengajuan-bs.attachment', $bs))->assertNotFound();
    }

    public function test_attachment_is_removed_when_the_submission_audit_fails(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        BsActivityLog::creating(fn () => throw new \RuntimeException('Attachment test audit failure'));
        try {
            $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))->assertStatus(500);
        } finally {
            BsActivityLog::flushEventListeners();
        }
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 0);
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public function test_attachment_is_removed_when_the_outer_transaction_fails_after_controller_success(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        Route::post('/attachment-outer-failure', function (Request $request) {
            app(PengajuanBsController::class)->store($request, app(BonSementaraService::class));
            return response('Simulated failure after saving', 500);
        })->middleware(['web', 'auth', 'bs.atomic'])->name('pengajuan-bs.attachment-test');
        $this->post('/attachment-outer-failure', $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))->assertStatus(500);
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 0);
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public function test_attachment_is_removed_after_inner_rollback_even_when_validation_redirect_commits_outer_transaction(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        BsActivityLog::creating(fn () => throw \Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException::withMessages(['error' => 'Simulated validation failure']));
        try {
            $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))->assertSessionHasErrors('error');
        } finally {
            BsActivityLog::flushEventListeners();
        }
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 0);
        $this->assertCount(0, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public function test_attachment_survives_a_retried_outer_transaction_without_extra_files(): void
    {
        Storage::fake(BsAttachmentService::DISK);
        $attempts = 0;
        Route::post('/attachment-retry', function (Request $request) use (&$attempts) {
            $response = app(PengajuanBsController::class)->store($request, app(BonSementaraService::class));
            if (++$attempts === 1) {
                throw new \Illuminate\Database\DeadlockException('Deadlock found when trying to get lock');
            }
            return $response;
        })->middleware(['web', 'auth', 'bs.atomic'])->name('pengajuan-bs.attachment-retry-test');
        // Let the retryable exception reach the transaction instead of rendering an HTTP 500.
        $this->withoutExceptionHandling();
        $this->post('/attachment-retry', $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertSame(2, $attempts);
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 1);
        Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->assertExists(PengajuanBs::firstOrFail()->attachment_path);
        $this->assertCount(1, Storage::disk(BsAttachmentService::DISK)->allFiles());
    }

    public static function attachmentStorageFailures(): array
    {
        return ['false result' => [false], 'write exception' => [true]];
    }

    #[DataProvider('attachmentStorageFailures')]
    public function test_attachment_storage_failure_prevents_submission(bool $throws): void
    {
        $disk = \Mockery::mock(\Illuminate\Filesystem\FilesystemAdapter::class);
        $write = $disk->shouldReceive('putFileAs')->once();
        if ($throws) {
            $write->andThrow(\League\Flysystem\UnableToWriteFile::atLocation('test.pdf', 'Simulated write failure'));
        } else {
            $write->andReturn(false);
        }
        Storage::set(BsAttachmentService::DISK, $disk);
        $this->post(route('pengajuan-bs.store'), $this->attachmentPayload($this->travelPdf()))->assertSessionHasErrors('attachment');
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('pengajuan_bs', 0);
    }
}

<?php

namespace Tests\Concerns;

use App\Models\BsUsageCheck;
use App\Models\BsUsageCheckDraft;
use App\Models\Periode;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\BonSementaraService;
use App\Services\BsCashFlowService;
use App\Services\BsPeriodClosingService;
use App\Services\BsUsageCheckService;
use App\Services\StockCashFlowService;
use App\Support\Money;

trait FinanceCashPostingScenarios
{
    public function test_finance_cash_opening_continues_published_september_closing_and_preserves_archives(): void
    {
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 10, 7));
        Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->update(['status' => 'CLOSED']);
        Periode::create(['periode' => '2026-10', 'status' => 'OPEN']);
        $oldBs = \App\Models\BsCashFlowSnapshot::create([
            'user_id' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'saldo_awal' => 21100000, 'total_masuk' => 9200000,
            'total_keluar' => 11370000, 'saldo_akhir' => 18930000, 'opening_final' => true,
        ]);
        $oldStock = \App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot::create([
            'user_id' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'saldo_awal' => 19150000, 'total_masuk' => 9200000,
            'total_keluar_bs' => 11370000, 'total_event_luar' => 200000, 'total_keluar' => 11570000,
            'saldo_akhir' => 16780000, 'opening_final' => true,
        ]);
        $frozenBs = $oldBs->fresh()->getAttributes();
        $frozenStock = $oldStock->fresh()->getAttributes();
        $bs = $this->bs(['tanggal' => '2026-10-01', 'periode' => '2026-10', 'nominal_approved' => 1200000]);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '100000');
        $this->dashboardFinanceCheck($source, date: '2026-10-07');
        for ($refresh = 0; $refresh < 2; $refresh++) {
            $this->assertFinanceCash('100000.00', '17880000.00', period: '2026-10');
            $stock = app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot(1, '2026-10');
            $this->assertSame('16780000.00', $stock->saldo_awal);
        }
        $this->get('/cash-flow')->assertOk()->assertSee('Rp 16.780.000')->assertSee('Rp 17.880.000');
        $book = $this->cashFlowWorkbook('/cash-flow/202610/export', 'stok-kas-202610.xlsx');
        $this->assertEquals(16780000, $book->getSheet(0)->getCell('C2')->getValue());
        $this->assertSame($frozenBs, $oldBs->fresh()->getAttributes());
        $this->assertSame($frozenStock, $oldStock->fresh()->getAttributes());
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 11, 7));
        app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->close(Periode::where('periode', '2026-10')->value('id'));
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '17880000.00', period: '2026-11');
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_carries_zero_negative_and_unfinalized_stock_closings_by_owner(): void
    {
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 10, 7));
        Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->update(['status' => 'CLOSED']);
        foreach ([1 => '0.00', 3 => '-200000.25'] as $owner => $closing) {
            \App\Models\BsCashFlowSnapshot::create([
                'user_id' => $owner, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'saldo_akhir' => 900000, 'opening_final' => true,
            ]);
            \App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot::create([
                'user_id' => $owner, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'saldo_akhir' => $closing, 'opening_final' => true,
            ]);
            $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', $closing, $owner, '2026-10');
        }
        \App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot::where('user_id', 1)->where('periode', '2026-09')->update(['opening_final' => false]);
        $this->assertFalse(app(BsCashFlowService::class)->calculate(1, '2026-10')['opening_final']);
        Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->update(['status' => 'OPEN']);
        $this->assertSame('0.00', app(BsCashFlowService::class)->calculate(3, '2026-10')['saldo_awal']);
        $this->assertFalse(app(BsCashFlowService::class)->calculate(3, '2026-10')['opening_final']);
    }

    private function confirmUsageFinance($source): void
    {
        $source->refresh();
        $type = array_search($source::class, BsUsageCheckService::TYPES, true);
        $this->dashboardFinanceCheck($source, $type);
    }

    /** Seed report fixtures, including imported historical deficits, without the write workflow. */
    private function seedFinanceApprovals(string $period = '2026-09'): void
    {
        $posting = app(\App\Services\FinanceBsExpenseService::class);
        foreach (BsUsageCheckService::TYPES as $type => $model) {
            foreach ($posting->sources($type, $period)->get() as $source) {
                if (BsUsageCheckService::isEvent($source)) $source->forceFill(['status' => 'VALIDATED'])->save();
                if ($source->status !== 'VALIDATED' || $posting->owner($source) === null) continue;
                $snapshot = app(BsUsageCheckService::class)->snapshot($source);
                $check = BsUsageCheck::create([
                    'document_no' => 'CEKBS-FIXTURE-'.(BsUsageCheck::count() + 1),
                    'tanggal_pengecekan' => $period.'-16', 'periode' => $period,
                    'jenis_transaksi' => $type, 'transaksi_pemakaian_id' => $source->id,
                    'no_referensi' => $source->transaksi_id ?: $type.' #'.$source->id,
                    'user_id' => $source->user_id, 'user_name' => $source->user->nama,
                    'user_checker_id' => 2, 'checker_name' => 'Finance',
                    'source_fingerprint' => $snapshot['fingerprint'], 'source_revision' => $source->submission_version,
                    'keputusan' => 'APPROVED',
                ]);
                $check->details()->createMany($snapshot['items']->map(fn ($item) =>
                    $item + ['nominal_disetujui' => $item['nominal_asal']])->all());
            }
        }
    }

    private function assertFinanceCash(string $outgoing, string $balance, int $owner = 1, string $period = '2026-09'): void
    {
        $bs = app(BsCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot($owner, $period);
        $stock = app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot($owner, $period);
        $this->assertSame($outgoing, $bs->total_keluar);
        $this->assertSame($outgoing, $stock->total_keluar);
        $this->assertSame($balance, app(BonSementaraService::class)->getRealtimeSisaSaldo($owner, $period));
        $this->assertSame($balance, $stock->saldo_berjalan);
        $rows = app(StockCashFlowService::class)->details($owner, $period)['keluar'];
        $this->assertSame($outgoing, Money::decimal(collect($rows)->sum(fn ($row) => Money::minor($row['nominal']))));
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_user_confirmation_waits_and_all_four_modules_use_approved_amounts(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->bs();
        $sources = [
            'OPR' => $this->expense($bs, amount: '300000'),
            'PD' => $this->expense($bs, amount: '200000', travel: true),
            'EDK' => $this->recapEvent('EDK', '150000'),
            'ELK' => $this->recapEvent('ELK', '100000'),
        ];
        foreach ($sources as $type => $source) {
            if (in_array($type, ['EDK', 'ELK'], true)) $this->recapConfirm($source, $type);
            else {
                $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(1));
                app(\App\Services\BsValidationService::class)->finalize($source);
                $source->refresh();
            }
            $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        }
        foreach (['OPR' => '250000.25', 'PD' => '180000.25', 'EDK' => '120000.25', 'ELK' => '80000.25'] as $type => $amount) {
            $this->recapDecision($sources[$type], $type, 'APPROVED', $amount);
        }
        $this->assertFinanceCash('630001.00', '369999.00');
        $stock = app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot(1, '2026-09');
        $this->assertSame('430000.50', $stock->total_keluar_bs);
        $this->assertSame('250000.25', $stock->total_keluar_operasional);
        $this->assertSame('180000.25', $stock->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas);
        $this->assertSame('120000.25', $stock->total_event_dalam);
        $this->assertSame('80000.25', $stock->total_event_luar);
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(1));
        foreach (['/cash-flow', '/cash-flow/202609'] as $url) {
            $this->assertCashModuleCards($this->get($url)->assertOk()->getContent(), [
                'Operasional Sales' => 'Rp 250.000', 'Perjalanan Dinas' => 'Rp 180.000',
                'Event Dalam Kota' => 'Rp 120.000', 'Event Luar Kota' => 'Rp 80.000',
            ]);
        }
        foreach (['/cash-flow', '/cash-flow/202609', route('dashboard.transaksi')] as $url) {
            $this->get($url)->assertOk()->assertSee('Saldo BS')->assertSee('369.999')->assertSee('630.001');
        }
        $workbook = $this->cashFlowWorkbook('/cash-flow/202609/export', 'stok-kas-202609.xlsx');
        $this->assertEquals(630001, $workbook->getSheet(0)->getCell('H2')->getValue());
        $this->assertEquals(630001, array_sum(array_column($workbook->getSheetByName('Uang Keluar')->rangeToArray('A2:D5', null, false, false), 3)));
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_drafts_and_rechecks_replace_only_the_latest_confirmed_amount(): void
    {
        $source = $this->expense($this->bs(), status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '300000');
        $service = app(BsUsageCheckService::class);
        $data = ['save_action' => 'draft', 'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-10-01',
            'source_token' => $this->recapToken($source, 'OPR'),
            'review_token' => $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, null, null),
            'details' => [['source_detail_id' => $source->details()->first()->id, 'cost_category_id' => 1, 'nominal_disetujui' => '250000']]];
        $draft = $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
        $this->assertInstanceOf(BsUsageCheckDraft::class, $draft);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $data['save_action'] = 'confirm';
        $data['review_token'] = $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, null, $draft);
        $first = $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('250000.00', '750000.00');
        $data['save_action'] = 'draft';
        $data['details'][0]['nominal_disetujui'] = '200000';
        $data['review_token'] = $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, $first, null);
        $draft = $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('250000.00', '750000.00');
        $data['save_action'] = 'confirm';
        $data['review_token'] = $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, $first, $draft);
        $second = $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
        $this->assertEquals($first->id, $second->revision_of_id);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('200000.00', '800000.00');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '0');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '300000');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'REJECTED', '0');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_checks', 5);
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_check_drafts', 0);
        $this->assertSame('250000.00', $first->details()->first()->nominal_disetujui);
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_checks_available_funds_at_finance_confirmation_and_allows_replacement(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->bs();
        $one = $this->expense($bs, amount: '600000');
        $two = $this->expense($bs, amount: '600000', travel: true);
        foreach ([$one, $two] as $source) app(\App\Services\BsValidationService::class)->finalize($source);
        $this->recapDecision($one, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '600000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('600000.00', '400000.00');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->postJson(route('bs-usage-checks.store', ['PD', $two->id]), [
            'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-09-16', 'source_token' => $this->recapToken($two, 'PD'),
            'details' => [['source_detail_id' => $two->details()->first()->id, 'cost_category_id' => 1, 'nominal_disetujui' => '600000']],
        ])->assertUnprocessable()->assertJsonValidationErrors('bs_balance');
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_checks', 1);
        $this->recapDecision($one, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '700000');
        $this->recapDecision($two, 'PD', 'APPROVED', '300000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('1000000.00', '0.00');
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_group_trip_posts_to_bs_owner_only_after_finance(): void
    {
        $source = $this->pendingMemberUsage('300000');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(1));
        app(\App\Services\BsValidationService::class)->approve($source, $source->approval_version);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'PD', 'APPROVED', '250000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('250000.00', '750000.00');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '0.00', 3);
        $this->assertSame('250000.00', app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot(1, '2026-09')->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas);
        $this->assertSame('0.00', app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot(3, '2026-09')->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas);
    }

    private function assertCashModuleCards(string $html, array $expected): void
    {
        $previous = libxml_use_internal_errors(true);
        $dom = new \DOMDocument();
        $dom->loadHTML($html);
        libxml_clear_errors();
        libxml_use_internal_errors($previous);
        $xpath = new \DOMXPath($dom);
        $cards = $xpath->query('(//div[contains(concat(" ", normalize-space(@class), " "), " bd-grid ")])[1]/div');
        $values = [];
        foreach ($cards as $card) {
            $spans = $xpath->query('./span', $card);
            $values[trim($spans[0]->textContent)] = trim($spans[1]->textContent);
        }
        foreach ($expected as $label => $value) $this->assertSame($value, $values[$label] ?? null, $label);
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_pd_cards_show_170000_from_two_items_and_follow_latest_confirmation(): void
    {
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 10, 12));
        Periode::create(['periode' => '2026-10', 'status' => 'OPEN']);
        $bs = $this->bs(['tanggal' => '2026-10-12', 'periode' => '2026-10', 'nominal_approved' => '250000']);
        $source = $this->expense($bs, status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '100000', travel: true);
        $source->details()->create(['cost_category_id' => 1, 'remark' => 'Cek oli', 'nominal' => '100000']);
        $items = $source->details()->orderBy('id')->get();
        $service = app(BsUsageCheckService::class);
        foreach (['confirm' => '70000', 'draft' => '50000', 'confirm_again' => '50000'] as $action => $secondAmount) {
            $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'PD', $source->id, [
                'save_action' => $action === 'draft' ? 'draft' : 'confirm',
                'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-10-12', 'source_token' => $this->recapToken($source, 'PD'),
                'review_token' => $service->reviewToken('PD', $source->id, $source->financeChecks()->latest('id')->first(),
                    BsUsageCheckDraft::where('jenis_transaksi', 'PD')->where('transaksi_pemakaian_id', $source->id)->first()),
                'details' => $items->map(fn ($item, $index) => [
                    'source_detail_id' => $item->id, 'cost_category_id' => 1,
                    'nominal_disetujui' => $index === 0 ? '100000' : $secondAmount,
                ])->all(),
            ]);
            $expected = $action === 'confirm_again' ? '150000.00' : '170000.00';
            $stock = app(StockCashFlowService::class)->recalculateSnapshot(1, '2026-10');
            $this->assertSame($expected, $stock->total_keluar);
            $this->assertSame($expected, $stock->total_keluar_perjalanan_dinas);
            $this->assertSame('0.00', $stock->total_keluar_operasional);
            foreach (['/cash-flow', '/cash-flow/202610'] as $url) {
                $this->assertCashModuleCards($this->get($url)->assertOk()->getContent(), [
                    'Pencairan BS' => 'Rp 250.000', 'Operasional Sales' => 'Rp 0',
                    'Perjalanan Dinas' => 'Rp '.number_format($expected, 0, ',', '.'),
                    'Event Dalam Kota' => 'Rp 0', 'Event Luar Kota' => 'Rp 0',
                ]);
            }
        }
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_legacy_breakdown_does_not_rewrite_closed_snapshots(): void
    {
        $bs = $this->bs();
        $this->expense($bs, status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '30000');
        $this->expense($bs, status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '170000', travel: true);
        Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->update(['status' => 'CLOSED']);
        $frozen = \App\Models\StockCashFlowSnapshot::create([
            'user_id' => 1, 'periode' => '2026-09', 'saldo_awal' => '100000', 'total_masuk' => '1000000',
            'total_keluar_bs' => '200000', 'total_event_dalam' => '10000', 'total_keluar' => '210000',
            'saldo_akhir' => '890000', 'opening_final' => true,
        ]);
        $before = $frozen->fresh()->getAttributes();
        foreach (['/cash-flow', '/cash-flow/202609'] as $url) {
            $this->assertCashModuleCards($this->get($url)->assertOk()->getContent(), [
                'Operasional Sales' => 'Rp 30.000', 'Perjalanan Dinas' => 'Rp 170.000',
            ]);
        }
        $this->assertSame($before, $frozen->fresh()->getAttributes());
        (require database_path('migrations/2026_10_09_032839_fix_operasional_perjalanan_dinas_nullable.php'))->up();
        $this->assertSame($before, $frozen->fresh()->getAttributes());
        $frozen->update(['total_keluar_bs' => '250000']);
        $this->assertCashModuleCards($this->get('/cash-flow')->assertOk()->getContent(), [
            'Operasional Sales' => 'Belum tersedia', 'Perjalanan Dinas' => 'Belum tersedia',
        ]);
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_close_waits_for_review_then_rolls_over_once_and_rejects_closed_recheck(): void
    {
        $this->bs();
        $event = $this->recapEvent('EDK', '300000');
        $this->recapConfirm($event, 'EDK');
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 10, 2));
        $period = Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->firstOrFail();
        try {
            app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->close($period->id);
            $this->fail('Unreviewed event must block closing.');
        } catch (\Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException $exception) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString('Finance', $exception->getMessage());
        }
        $this->recapDecision($event, 'EDK', 'APPROVED', '250000');
        app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->close($period->id);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('250000.00', '750000.00');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '750000.00', period: '2026-10');
        $this->actingAs(User::findOrFail(2));
        $this->postJson(route('bs-usage-checks.store', ['EDK', $event->id]), [
            'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-10-02', 'source_token' => $this->recapToken($event, 'EDK'),
            'details' => [['source_detail_id' => null, 'cost_category_id' => null, 'nominal_disetujui' => '200000']],
        ])->assertUnprocessable()->assertJsonValidationErrors('tanggal');
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_checks', 1);
        app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->reopen($period->id, 'Koreksi Finance');
        $this->recapDecision($event, 'EDK', 'APPROVED', '200000');
        app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->close($period->id);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '800000.00', period: '2026-10');
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_rolls_back_check_and_keeps_draft_when_snapshot_write_fails(): void
    {
        $source = $this->expense($this->bs(), status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '300000');
        $service = app(BsUsageCheckService::class);
        $data = ['save_action' => 'draft', 'tanggal_pengecekan' => '2026-09-16',
            'source_token' => $this->recapToken($source, 'OPR'),
            'review_token' => $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, null, null),
            'details' => [['source_detail_id' => $source->details()->first()->id, 'cost_category_id' => 1, 'nominal_disetujui' => '250000']]];
        $draft = $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
        $data['save_action'] = 'confirm';
        $data['review_token'] = $service->reviewToken('OPR', $source->id, null, $draft);
        $this->mock(StockCashFlowService::class)->shouldReceive('recalculateSnapshot')->once()->andThrow(new \RuntimeException('Snapshot failed'));
        try {
            $service->store(User::findOrFail(2), 'OPR', $source->id, $data);
            $this->fail('Confirmation should roll back.');
        } catch (\RuntimeException $exception) {
            $this->assertSame('Snapshot failed', $exception->getMessage());
        }
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_checks', 0);
        $this->assertDatabaseCount('bs_usage_check_drafts', 1);
        $this->app->forgetInstance(StockCashFlowService::class);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
    }

    public function test_finance_cash_stale_versions_and_changed_categories_wait_for_recheck(): void
    {
        $source = $this->expense($this->bs(), status: 'VALIDATED', amount: '300000');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '250000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('250000.00', '750000.00');
        $source->forceFill(['submission_version' => (int) $source->submission_version + 1])->save();
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '200000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('200000.00', '800000.00');
        \Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB::table('cost_category')->insert(['id' => 2, 'nama' => 'Makan']);
        $source->details()->first()->update(['cost_category_id' => 2]);
        $this->assertFinanceCash('0.00', '1000000.00');
        $this->travelTo(now()->setDate(2026, 10, 2));
        try {
            app(BsPeriodClosingService::class)->close(Periode::where('periode', '2026-09')->value('id'));
            $this->fail('A stale Finance check cannot finalize the period.');
        } catch (\Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException $exception) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString('Finance', $exception->getMessage());
        }
        $this->recapDecision($source, 'OPR', 'APPROVED', '150000');
        $this->assertFinanceCash('150000.00', '850000.00');
    }
}
